# Brick catalogue

Every brick of the library, 388 in all, grouped by category, then by use and arc slot, from each brick's selection fields (`select` in [the index](../assets/runtime/bricks/index.json)); generated, do not edit. Pick candidates for a slot here, then open their cards (the id links to it; each brick's folder `assets/runtime/bricks/<category>/<id>/` holds its code, card and poster). A brick with several slots is listed under each. Intent is what the viewer gets; the Chinese column says the same for Chinese search.

## Motion

### Seam (between two scenes)

#### Slot: hook

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`cursor-collapse`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/cursor-collapse/CARD.md) | Cursor collapse | Return the screen to its source: everything goes back into the cursor, which becomes the point the next shot grows from. | 让屏幕回到源头：一切回到光标里，光标变成下一个镜头长出来的点。 | cursor title | dramatic cinematic | high | 4 beats |
| [`lamp-reveal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/lamp-reveal/CARD.md) | Lamp reveal | In the dark the viewer sees only what the light gives, so the lamp decides what appears and when: lighting as camera and edit. | 黑暗里观众只看得见光给的东西，由灯决定什么登场、何时登场：光就是运镜和剪辑。 | title ui abstract | cinematic dramatic calm | mid | 9.1 beats |
| [`light-leak`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/light-leak/CARD.md) | Light leak | A warm, invisible cut: the eye is blinded by a sweep of film light and when it clears the scene has changed, which reads as time passing. | 温暖、看不见剪刀的切换：一团胶片漏光晃住眼睛，光退去时景已经换了，读作时间流过。 | abstract | warm cinematic | mid | 4.8 beats |
| [`perspective-glide`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/perspective-glide/CARD.md) | Perspective glide | The page shows itself off: a long interface stands as a steep wall and slides past a still camera like train carriages, so it reads as a real object in space. | 页面在自我展示：长界面立成一堵斜墙，像列车车厢一样从不动的镜头前滑过，读成空间里的实物。 | ui space | cinematic technical | mid | 8 beats |
| [`shard-gather`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/shard-gather/CARD.md) | Shard gather | Film 'the product takes shape' as a physical event: a whole picture grows out of disorder before the viewer's eyes; reversed, it clears the screen by flying apart, not fading. | 把“产品成形”拍成一件物理事件：观众看着一整面画面从无序中长出来；反着用时，画面是被炸飞出画，而不是淡掉。 | image title | dramatic energetic cinematic | high | 6.3 beats |
| [`tilt-camera`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tilt-camera/CARD.md) | Tilt camera | The camera's pitch tells the story: a page lifted from the table into view, a pick from a desk of pages, or a dive from above onto the hero, each landing on one upright page. | 用俯仰角讲故事：像掀开桌上的图纸把一页抬到眼前，或在一桌页面里巡一圈挑中一张，或从高空俯冲落到主角上，最后都落在一张正立的页面。 | ui space | cinematic dramatic | high | 4.5 beats |
| [`tumble-stair`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tumble-stair/CARD.md) | Tumble stair | A set of pages appears out of nothing and lines up with energy: cards flip in from their thin edges into a stair, showing "many capabilities" at a glance. | 一组页面从无到有、带着冲劲列队：卡片从薄边翻飞成阶梯，一眼看到“好多能力”。 | cards ui | energetic cinematic | high | 3.8 beats |

#### Slot: problem

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`page-turn`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/page-turn/CARD.md) | Page turn | The next evidence was under this one all along: the change is paper being handled, not a cut. | 下一份证据一直压在这份下面：换页是翻纸，不是剪辑。 | image text | editorial calm | mid | 1 beats |
| [`term-wall`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/term-wall/CARD.md) | Terminal to wall | Show scale by multiplication: the one window the viewer knows becomes a wall too big to read one by one. | 用复制来表现规模：观众认识的那一个窗口变成一面读不过来的墙。 | terminal device | technical calm | mid | 4 beats |

#### Slot: turn

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`bar-window`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/bar-window/CARD.md) | Bar window | The cut is caused by the dot: the last thing standing opens the next scene. | 切换是墨点引起的：最后留在画面上的东西打开了下一场。 | abstract | energetic mechanical | mid | 1 beats |
| [`block-carry`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/block-carry/CARD.md) | Block carry | Keep one solid shape across the cut so two pages read as one argument; the colour keeps its meaning (ink the system, accent the person). | 让一个实心形状跨过剪辑点，两页读起来是同一个论证；颜色的含义不变（墨色是系统，强调色是人）。 | abstract title | editorial mechanical | mid | 2 beats |
| [`card-portal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/card-portal/CARD.md) | Card portal | Clicking into one item of an overview: the viewer feels they have entered that card's own world, not watched a wipe. | 像点开总览里的一张卡走进它的世界：观众感到是“进入这一项”，而不是看了一次擦除。 | cards ui | cinematic elegant | mid | 4 beats |
| [`chapter-push`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/chapter-push/CARD.md) | Chapter push | The new chapter shoves the old one out as one solid slab, so the viewer reads "a new world" with a physical clunk; repeated, the pushes become the film's chapter rhythm. | 新章像一整块板把旧章顶出画外，观众读到“换了一个世界”，还带一记落位的“哐”；连用时换章本身成了全片的节拍。 | abstract cards | energetic editorial | mid | 1.8 beats |
| [`cursor-collapse`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/cursor-collapse/CARD.md) | Cursor collapse | Return the screen to its source: everything goes back into the cursor, which becomes the point the next shot grows from. | 让屏幕回到源头：一切回到光标里，光标变成下一个镜头长出来的点。 | cursor title | dramatic cinematic | high | 4 beats |
| [`dot-quantize`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/dot-quantize/CARD.md) | Dot quantize | A signal becomes data before the viewer's eyes: the same picture is reprinted as blocks and re-sorted into the next one. | 信号在观众眼前变成数据：同一张画面被重印成方块，再重排成下一张。 | abstract | technical mechanical | mid | 2 beats |
| [`frame-snap`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/frame-snap/CARD.md) | Frame snap | The colour of the frame becomes the code for a mode, so one hard flip says "we switched modes" without touching the content. | 画框颜色成了模式的代号，一次硬翻色就说清“换模式了”，不用动内容本身。 | ui device | mechanical energetic | high | 3.6 beats |
| [`glitch-tear`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/glitch-tear/CARD.md) | Glitch tear | The system switches state in one jolt: the page tears into bands but stays whole, so the viewer reads a digital jump, not a broken screen. | 系统一下子跳到新状态：页面撕成横条但每条完整，观众读到的是数字式跳变，而不是页面坏了。 | ui abstract | energetic technical | high | 2.1 beats |
| [`ground-flip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/ground-flip/CARD.md) | Ground flip | The whole ground drops from light to dark after a long build, so the viewer feels the story turn before reading the next line. | 长长的铺垫之后整片底色由亮压暗，让观众在读到下一句之前先感到故事转了。 | title abstract | dramatic cinematic | peak | 10.4 beats |
| [`layer-zoom`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/layer-zoom/CARD.md) | Layer zoom | Keep the viewer sure which overview cell became which detail part. | 让观众确定总图里的哪一格变成了细节里的哪个部件。 | diagram | technical calm | mid | 2.6 beats |
| [`light-leak`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/light-leak/CARD.md) | Light leak | A warm, invisible cut: the eye is blinded by a sweep of film light and when it clears the scene has changed, which reads as time passing. | 温暖、看不见剪刀的切换：一团胶片漏光晃住眼睛，光退去时景已经换了，读作时间流过。 | abstract | warm cinematic | mid | 4.8 beats |
| [`line-carry`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/line-carry/CARD.md) | Line carry | No cut at all: one line, born from something finishing, leads the eye across to the next place and draws its frame, so two scenes read as one gesture. | 根本不切：一条从“走完了”的东西里长出的线牵着视线走到下一处，并画出它的框，两场读作一笔。 | diagram abstract | editorial elegant | mid | 9.2 beats |
| [`outline-morph`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/outline-morph/CARD.md) | Outline morph | The viewer sees one thing change form, not a cut to another thing, so the new form inherits the old one's meaning. | 观众看到的是一样东西换了形状，而不是切到另一样东西，新形状继承了旧形状的意义。 | abstract ui | elegant technical | mid | 1 beats |
| [`pose-fly`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/pose-fly/CARD.md) | Pose fly | Turning the page becomes travelling through a space of ideas: each card has its own place and angle, so arriving at it feels like entering a new chapter. | 把“翻页”变成在想法的空间里旅行：每张卡有自己的位置和角度，飞到它面前就像进入新的一章。 | cards space | cinematic elegant | mid | 2 beats |
| [`route-pan`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/route-pan/CARD.md) | Route pan | Keep one space: the camera travels the drawing and the route line pulls the eye to the next sheet, so nothing is a cut. | 保持同一个空间：镜头在图上移动，路线把视线拉向下一张，没有一处是硬切。 | diagram space | technical calm | mid | 1 beats |
| [`tilt-camera`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tilt-camera/CARD.md) | Tilt camera | The camera's pitch tells the story: a page lifted from the table into view, a pick from a desk of pages, or a dive from above onto the hero, each landing on one upright page. | 用俯仰角讲故事：像掀开桌上的图纸把一页抬到眼前，或在一桌页面里巡一圈挑中一张，或从高空俯冲落到主角上，最后都落在一张正立的页面。 | ui space | cinematic dramatic | high | 4.5 beats |
| [`title-demote`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/title-demote/CARD.md) | Title demote | The title steps down instead of leaving, so the viewer keeps a free spatial memory: the big word from a moment ago is the small label up there, and this section is about it. | 标题降格而不退场，观众白得一份空间记忆：刚才的大字就是左上角那个小标签，这一节讲的就是它。 | title ui | editorial calm | low | 4 beats |
| [`unit-flight`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/unit-flight/CARD.md) | Unit flight | The same items move from one chart into the next, so the viewer trusts that every chapter counts the same things. | 同一批方块从一张图飞进下一张图，观众因此相信每一章数的都是同一批东西。 | chart | technical calm | mid | 5 beats |

| [`time-freeze`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/time-freeze/CARD.md) | Time freeze | "This moment is worth stopping time for": the content freezes while we look at it from the side or mark it up, so one instant of a process lands with weight. | “这一刻值得停下时间看”：内容冻住，镜头绕到侧面看或在上面圈注，过程中的一瞬被郑重点出来。 | chart metric ui | dramatic cinematic | mid | 9.5 beats |

| [`word-to-chip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/word-to-chip/CARD.md) | Word to chip | Text becomes a thing the product understands: a hard cut gives a sure "done", and the one glide between two cuts makes it feel quick and certain. | 文字变成产品认得的实体：硬切给出“啪、成了”的确定感，两次硬切夹一次滑动显得又快又稳。 | ui text | technical energetic | mid | 3 beats |

| [`tilt-camera`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tilt-camera/CARD.md) | Tilt camera | The camera's pitch tells the story: a page lifted from the table into view, a pick from a desk of pages, or a dive from above onto the hero, each landing on one upright page. | 用俯仰角讲故事：像掀开桌上的图纸把一页抬到眼前，或在一桌页面里巡一圈挑中一张，或从高空俯冲落到主角上，最后都落在一张正立的页面。 | ui space | cinematic dramatic | high | 4.5 beats |
| [`time-freeze`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/time-freeze/CARD.md) | Time freeze | "This moment is worth stopping time for": the content freezes while we look at it from the side or mark it up, so one instant of a process lands with weight. | “这一刻值得停下时间看”：内容冻住，镜头绕到侧面看或在上面圈注，过程中的一瞬被郑重点出来。 | chart metric ui | dramatic cinematic | mid | 9.5 beats |
| [`tumble-stair`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tumble-stair/CARD.md) | Tumble stair | A set of pages appears out of nothing and lines up with energy: cards flip in from their thin edges into a stair, showing "many capabilities" at a glance. | 一组页面从无到有、带着冲劲列队：卡片从薄边翻飞成阶梯，一眼看到“好多能力”。 | cards ui | energetic cinematic | high | 3.8 beats |

| [`bar-window`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/bar-window/CARD.md) | Bar window | The cut is caused by the dot: the last thing standing opens the next scene. | 切换是墨点引起的：最后留在画面上的东西打开了下一场。 | abstract | energetic mechanical | mid | 1 beats |
| [`crop-cuts`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/crop-cuts/CARD.md) | Crop cuts | "Look here, closer, this one": hard cuts to ever tighter crops force the eye onto one thing; with flashes it becomes the film's loudest drumbeat. | “看这里、再近点、就是它”：一刀比一刀紧的硬切逼着观众盯住一个东西；加白闪时就是全片最响的一记鼓点。 | ui image metric | energetic dramatic | mid | 5 beats |
| [`layout-morph`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/layout-morph/CARD.md) | Layout morph | The layout itself is thinking: the same set of items re-sorts into a new arrangement while the eye follows each one, so the viewer sees one collection read another way. | 让观众看见“布局在思考”：同一批内容换一种排法，每一片都能被眼睛跟到新位置，读到“同样的东西，另一种看法”。 | image cards | elegant calm | mid | 2.8 beats |
| [`page-turn`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/page-turn/CARD.md) | Page turn | The next evidence was under this one all along: the change is paper being handled, not a cut. | 下一份证据一直压在这份下面：换页是翻纸，不是剪辑。 | image text | editorial calm | mid | 1 beats |
| [`sheet-deal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/sheet-deal/CARD.md) | Sheet deal | Change the evidence without moving the camera: one sheet out, the next dealt on where the eye already is. | 不动镜头换证据：一张出去，下一张发到视线已经在的地方。 | image text | editorial calm | low | 1.5 beats |
| [`time-freeze`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/time-freeze/CARD.md) | Time freeze | "This moment is worth stopping time for": the content freezes while we look at it from the side or mark it up, so one instant of a process lands with weight. | “这一刻值得停下时间看”：内容冻住，镜头绕到侧面看或在上面圈注，过程中的一瞬被郑重点出来。 | chart metric ui | dramatic cinematic | mid | 9.5 beats |
| [`unit-flight`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/unit-flight/CARD.md) | Unit flight | The same items move from one chart into the next, so the viewer trusts that every chapter counts the same things. | 同一批方块从一张图飞进下一张图，观众因此相信每一章数的都是同一批东西。 | chart | technical calm | mid | 5 beats |

#### Slot: hook

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`attention-hop`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/attention-hop/CARD.md) | Attention hop | A new feature asks to be noticed, louder each time, so the viewer looks at it before it opens. | 一个新功能一次比一次大声地讨关注，让观众在它打开之前先把目光投过去。 | logo ui | playful energetic | mid | 8 beats |
| [`brace-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/brace-open/CARD.md) | Brace open | The name is "bracketed out" as the content itself, so the viewer reads it as the thing being defined, with a developer's accent. | 名字被一对括号“括出来”，让观众把它读成被定义的那个东西本身，自带开发者语感。 | title logo code | technical elegant | mid | 5 beats |
| [`card-conjure`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/card-conjure/CARD.md) | Card conjure | Make the one hero card feel conjured out of nothing: a flash marks where to look, the card is thrown from it and stops dead face-on, a magician's production that gives the object ceremony. | 让唯一的主角卡像被变出来：一道闪光先告诉观众看哪里，卡从光里被甩出、硬定格正对镜头，魔术师式的登场给这个物件仪式感。 | cards image | dramatic energetic | high | 5.4 beats |
| [`caret-fall`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/caret-fall/CARD.md) | Caret falls flat | Keep one object on screen as it changes job, without crossing the words being read. | 让同一个物体换工作时一直在画面上，而且不越过正在读的字。 | cursor text | calm technical | low | 1 beats |
| [`cursor-collapse`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/cursor-collapse/CARD.md) | Cursor collapse | Return the screen to its source: everything goes back into the cursor, which becomes the point the next shot grows from. | 让屏幕回到源头：一切回到光标里，光标变成下一个镜头长出来的点。 | cursor title | dramatic cinematic | high | 4 beats |
| [`dial-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/dial-sweep/CARD.md) | Dial sweep | The number is shown inside a range, sweeping from many down to few, so it argues for itself; then the same figure becomes the title. | 把数字放进一段量程里，从很多一路扫到很少，数字自己就是论证；停下后同一个数字变成标题。 | number title | energetic mechanical | high | 5.5 beats |
| [`float-land`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/float-land/CARD.md) | Float land | The interface completes itself as you look: its words hover with shadows and settle into place, so the viewer feels the UI arrive rather than fade in. | 界面在观众眼前“自己完成”：文字带着影子悬在上空再逐条落定，读到的是界面到位，而不是淡入。 | ui list text | elegant calm | low | 3.5 beats |
| [`glyph-decode`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/glyph-decode/CARD.md) | Glyph decode | Let a line resolve the way a terminal draws it, so it is read as it locks. | 让一行字像终端那样“解”出来，边锁定边被读到。 | title code | technical mysterious | low | 2 beats |
| [`glyph-slam`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/glyph-slam/CARD.md) | Glyph slam | Each word is a hit you can see: big to small, blur to sharp, with a printing ghost. | 每个词都是看得见的一下重击：由大到小、由糊到清，带着印刷残影。 | title text | energetic dramatic | high | 1 beats |
| [`gradient-drift`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/gradient-drift/CARD.md) | Gradient drift | Keeps a still layout alive: a slow, ever-moving ground gives text-only passages a quiet pulse without pulling the eye. | 让静止排版“活着”：缓慢变化的底色给纯文字段落一层安静的生气，又不抢视线。 | abstract | calm elegant | low | 12 beats |
| [`guide-letter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/guide-letter/CARD.md) | Guide lettering | Letters are drafted, not placed: guide lines first, then the word traced in reading order. | 字是画出来的，不是摆上去的：先打构造线，再按阅读顺序写出。 | title text | technical elegant | low | 3 beats |
| [`lamp-reveal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/lamp-reveal/CARD.md) | Lamp reveal | In the dark the viewer sees only what the light gives, so the lamp decides what appears and when: lighting as camera and edit. | 黑暗里观众只看得见光给的东西，由灯决定什么登场、何时登场：光就是运镜和剪辑。 | title ui abstract | cinematic dramatic calm | mid | 9.1 beats |
| [`lead-word`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/lead-word/CARD.md) | Lead word | Reading order becomes physical order: the viewer is made to read the lead word alone first, then sees the sentence grow round it, and has read the subject twice. | 把阅读顺序做成物理顺序：观众被迫先单独读首词，再看整句围绕它长出来，落定时主词已经读了两遍。 | title logo | energetic cinematic | high | 3.5 beats |
| [`needle-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/needle-sweep/CARD.md) | Needle sweep | Show the full scale before the reading, so the viewer feels how big the number is; a ceremony of systems coming online. | 先亮量程再报数，让观众感到数字有多大；系统就绪的开场仪式感。 | metric | mechanical ceremonial | high | 4 beats |
| [`outline-write`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/outline-write/CARD.md) | Outline write | Let a name feel composed rather than announced: one invisible hand writes every letter at once, so the word arrives as one calm act. | 让一个名字像被从容写成，而不是被喊出来：一只看不见的手同时写所有字，整个词作为一次安静的动作出现。 | title logo | calm elegant ceremonial | low | 4.4 beats |
| [`perspective-fling`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/perspective-fling/CARD.md) | Perspective fling | The hero card arrives with flair: the bent lens says speed and nearness, and the snap flat says "it's here". | 主角卡潇洒登场：夸张透视说的是快和近，“啪”地拍平说的是“它来了”。 | cards | energetic playful dramatic | high | 2.4 beats |
| [`perspective-glide`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/perspective-glide/CARD.md) | Perspective glide | The page shows itself off: a long interface stands as a steep wall and slides past a still camera like train carriages, so it reads as a real object in space. | 页面在自我展示：长界面立成一堵斜墙，像列车车厢一样从不动的镜头前滑过，读成空间里的实物。 | ui space | cinematic technical | mid | 8 beats |
| [`plate-slip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/plate-slip/CARD.md) | Plate slip | A hit in the language of print: the plates slip out of register and snap back, so the landing feels like ink stamped on paper, not a screen glitch. | 用印刷的语言打一拳：两块印版错位再“啪”地套准，命中像在纸上盖了一枚油墨章，而不是屏幕故障。 | title | editorial energetic playful | high | 4 beats |
| [`reel-in`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/reel-in/CARD.md) | Reel in | Make taking everything over feel like one effortless pull. | 让“全部接手”像轻松的一拉。 | abstract ui | energetic playful | high | 3 beats |
| [`segment-reveal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/segment-reveal/CARD.md) | Segment reveal | Hold back recognition to make a hook: the viewer sees only mysterious pieces until the key ones land, and the moment the word is recognised is the payoff. | 把“认出来”压到最后做成钩子：观众前大半时间只看到神秘的碎段，钥匙段落位的那一刻认出是什么字，这一下就是回报。 | title number logo | dramatic cinematic | mid | 8.5 beats |
| [`shard-gather`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/shard-gather/CARD.md) | Shard gather | Film 'the product takes shape' as a physical event: a whole picture grows out of disorder before the viewer's eyes; reversed, it clears the screen by flying apart, not fading. | 把“产品成形”拍成一件物理事件：观众看着一整面画面从无序中长出来；反着用时，画面是被炸飞出画，而不是淡掉。 | image title | dramatic energetic cinematic | high | 6.3 beats |

| [`stagger-in`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/stagger-in/CARD.md) | Stagger in | Let a set be read in order while the screen stays alive; in the bars and dots sets the order itself becomes visible, as a gradient or as a wave of power coming on. | 让一组东西按顺序被读到，同时画面一直是活的；柱和点阵两式里，顺序本身也看得见：一道梯度，或一次从源头扩散开的通电。 | list chart abstract | calm technical | low | 8 beats |
| [`thread-pierce`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/thread-pierce/CARD.md) | Thread pierce | Show one attention taking every loose thing at once. | 让人看到一个注意力一次接住了所有散乱的东西。 | abstract ui | dramatic energetic | high | 5 beats |

| [`type-enter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-enter/CARD.md) | Type enter | The title arrives piece by piece in reading order, so the viewer reads it as it lands; blur stays out of the way, the other ways make the arrival an event. | 标题按阅读顺序一块块到位，观众边落边读；blur 不抢戏，其余几种把入场本身做成一件事。 | title text | calm elegant playful | mid | 3 beats |
| [`type-retype`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-retype/CARD.md) | Type and retype | A small drama of hesitate, reject, declare: the viewer sees the plain word thrown out and the real claim typed faster, so the claim reads as a decision. | 一出“犹豫、否定、宣言”的小戏：观众看着平庸的说法被删掉、真正的卖点被更快地打出来，主张读成一个决定。 | text title terminal | playful technical | mid | 4 beats |

| [`ground-flip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/ground-flip/CARD.md) | Ground flip | The whole ground drops from light to dark after a long build, so the viewer feels the story turn before reading the next line. | 长长的铺垫之后整片底色由亮压暗，让观众在读到下一句之前先感到故事转了。 | title abstract | dramatic cinematic | peak | 10.4 beats |
| [`hand-mark`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/hand-mark/CARD.md) | Hand mark | Point like a person at the one thing that matters, so the viewer knows where to look. | 像人用手指一样指向那一件要紧的事，让观众知道看哪里。 | text abstract | warm editorial | low | 1.5 beats |

| [`outline-morph`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/outline-morph/CARD.md) | Outline morph | The viewer sees one thing change form, not a cut to another thing, so the new form inherits the old one's meaning. | 观众看到的是一样东西换了形状，而不是切到另一样东西，新形状继承了旧形状的意义。 | abstract ui | elegant technical | mid | 1 beats |

| [`split-fuse`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/split-fuse/CARD.md) | Split fuse | Show what the thing is made of, then make it one: each part is recognised on its own before the slam makes them the whole. | 先让人看清它由什么组成，再合成一个：两半各自被认出来，扣合那一下让它们成为整体。 | image logo | dramatic energetic cinematic | peak | 10 beats |
| [`theme-reveal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/theme-reveal/CARD.md) | Theme reveal | The same interface changes its skin before the viewer's eyes, so it reads as one product changing state, not a cut to another page. | 同一个界面在观众眼前换肤，读成一个产品换了状态，而不是切到另一页。 | ui | technical elegant | mid | 3.2 beats |
| [`tilt-camera`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tilt-camera/CARD.md) | Tilt camera | The camera's pitch tells the story: a page lifted from the table into view, a pick from a desk of pages, or a dive from above onto the hero, each landing on one upright page. | 用俯仰角讲故事：像掀开桌上的图纸把一页抬到眼前，或在一桌页面里巡一圈挑中一张，或从高空俯冲落到主角上，最后都落在一张正立的页面。 | ui space | cinematic dramatic | high | 4.5 beats |
| [`title-demote`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/title-demote/CARD.md) | Title demote | The title steps down instead of leaving, so the viewer keeps a free spatial memory: the big word from a moment ago is the small label up there, and this section is about it. | 标题降格而不退场，观众白得一份空间记忆：刚才的大字就是左上角那个小标签，这一节讲的就是它。 | title ui | editorial calm | low | 4 beats |
| [`type-enter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-enter/CARD.md) | Type enter | The title arrives piece by piece in reading order, so the viewer reads it as it lands; blur stays out of the way, the other ways make the arrival an event. | 标题按阅读顺序一块块到位，观众边落边读；blur 不抢戏，其余几种把入场本身做成一件事。 | title text | calm elegant playful | mid | 3 beats |
| [`type-retype`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-retype/CARD.md) | Type and retype | A small drama of hesitate, reject, declare: the viewer sees the plain word thrown out and the real claim typed faster, so the claim reads as a decision. | 一出“犹豫、否定、宣言”的小戏：观众看着平庸的说法被删掉、真正的卖点被更快地打出来，主张读成一个决定。 | text title terminal | playful technical | mid | 4 beats |
| [`unit-flight`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/unit-flight/CARD.md) | Unit flight | The same items move from one chart into the next, so the viewer trusts that every chapter counts the same things. | 同一批方块从一张图飞进下一张图，观众因此相信每一章数的都是同一批东西。 | chart | technical calm | mid | 5 beats |

| [`card-conjure`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/card-conjure/CARD.md) | Card conjure | Make the one hero card feel conjured out of nothing: a flash marks where to look, the card is thrown from it and stops dead face-on, a magician's production that gives the object ceremony. | 让唯一的主角卡像被变出来：一道闪光先告诉观众看哪里，卡从光里被甩出、硬定格正对镜头，魔术师式的登场给这个物件仪式感。 | cards image | dramatic energetic | high | 5.4 beats |
| [`card-squash`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/card-squash/CARD.md) | Card squash | Turn the finished thing back into the line, so the close can grow from it. | 把做好的东西变回那条线，让收尾从线里长出来。 | cards abstract | calm cinematic | mid | 1 beats |
| [`clone-echo`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/clone-echo/CARD.md) | Clone echo | Make 'one is many' visible: the viewer counts the copies of one thing, then watches them fold back into it, so scale reads as a property of a single product. | 把“一个就是很多个”变成看得见的事：观众先数清同一个东西有很多份，再看它们收回本体，规模感落在一个产品身上。 | cards ui | technical cinematic | mid | 6.4 beats |
| [`crop-cuts`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/crop-cuts/CARD.md) | Crop cuts | "Look here, closer, this one": hard cuts to ever tighter crops force the eye onto one thing; with flashes it becomes the film's loudest drumbeat. | “看这里、再近点、就是它”：一刀比一刀紧的硬切逼着观众盯住一个东西；加白闪时就是全片最响的一记鼓点。 | ui image metric | energetic dramatic | mid | 5 beats |
| [`dial-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/dial-sweep/CARD.md) | Dial sweep | The number is shown inside a range, sweeping from many down to few, so it argues for itself; then the same figure becomes the title. | 把数字放进一段量程里，从很多一路扫到很少，数字自己就是论证；停下后同一个数字变成标题。 | number title | energetic mechanical | high | 5.5 beats |
| [`dimension-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/dimension-line/CARD.md) | Dimension line | Measure the gap on the evidence itself, so the difference is seen, not asserted. | 在证据本身上量出差距，让差值被看见，而不是被宣称。 | chart number metric | technical dramatic | mid | 2 beats |
| [`explode-z`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/explode-z/CARD.md) | Explode z | "See what it is made of": the viewer sees a product's layers lift apart and click back, the Apple-style exploded view of its build. | “看看它由什么组成”：观众看到产品的各层升起分开再合回，苹果发布会式的爆炸图讲清构成。 | ui diagram space | cinematic technical | mid | 9.5 beats |
| [`ground-flip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/ground-flip/CARD.md) | Ground flip | The whole ground drops from light to dark after a long build, so the viewer feels the story turn before reading the next line. | 长长的铺垫之后整片底色由亮压暗，让观众在读到下一句之前先感到故事转了。 | title abstract | dramatic cinematic | peak | 10.4 beats |
| [`impact-frame`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/impact-frame/CARD.md) | Impact frame | The film's one hit lands with force: three negative frames bite into time, so the viewer feels the blow rather than sees a picture. | 全片唯一的一拳打实：三帧负片把时间咬住，观众感到的是冲击力，而不是看到一张图。 | cards number title | dramatic energetic cinematic | peak | 2.5 beats |
| [`isolate`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/isolate/CARD.md) | Isolate | "In the end, only this one thing": everything around one card goes dark as the camera pulls away, a quiet, heavy close on the point the film is for. | “最后只剩这一件事”：镜头后拉、周围逐张熄灭，安静而有分量地收在全片要说的那一点上。 | cards metric | dramatic calm | low | 7.5 beats |
| [`on-threes`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/on-threes/CARD.md) | On threes | The climax feels faster than anything before it: a hand-drawn, stepping move suddenly runs smooth, and the switch itself is the high point. | 高潮显得比之前任何时候都快：先是手绘式一顿一顿，突然变成逐帧丝滑，换拍这一下本身就是看点。 | abstract cards | playful energetic | high | 4 beats |
| [`panel-carve`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/panel-carve/CARD.md) | Panel carve | One subject seen from three angles at once, cut in on the beat like comic panels; the layout itself becomes the drum, then resolves into one poster. | 像漫画分格一样踩拍劈出同一主体的三个机位，格子本身成了鼓点，最后合成一张海报。 | image ui | energetic dramatic | high | 4 beats |
| [`pencil-loop`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/pencil-loop/CARD.md) | Pencil loop | A hand mark that reads as a choice, not as the system’s highlight. | 一道手迹，读起来是人的选择，而不是系统的高亮。 | image abstract | playful calm | mid | 2 beats |
| [`perspective-fling`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/perspective-fling/CARD.md) | Perspective fling | The hero card arrives with flair: the bent lens says speed and nearness, and the snap flat says "it's here". | 主角卡潇洒登场：夸张透视说的是快和近，“啪”地拍平说的是“它来了”。 | cards | energetic playful dramatic | high | 2.4 beats |
| [`plate-slip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/plate-slip/CARD.md) | Plate slip | A hit in the language of print: the plates slip out of register and snap back, so the landing feels like ink stamped on paper, not a screen glitch. | 用印刷的语言打一拳：两块印版错位再“啪”地套准，命中像在纸上盖了一枚油墨章，而不是屏幕故障。 | title | editorial energetic playful | high | 4 beats |
| [`reel-in`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/reel-in/CARD.md) | Reel in | Make taking everything over feel like one effortless pull. | 让“全部接手”像轻松的一拉。 | abstract ui | energetic playful | high | 3 beats |
| [`segment-reveal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/segment-reveal/CARD.md) | Segment reveal | Hold back recognition to make a hook: the viewer sees only mysterious pieces until the key ones land, and the moment the word is recognised is the payoff. | 把“认出来”压到最后做成钩子：观众前大半时间只看到神秘的碎段，钥匙段落位的那一刻认出是什么字，这一下就是回报。 | title number logo | dramatic cinematic | mid | 8.5 beats |
| [`shard-gather`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/shard-gather/CARD.md) | Shard gather | Film 'the product takes shape' as a physical event: a whole picture grows out of disorder before the viewer's eyes; reversed, it clears the screen by flying apart, not fading. | 把“产品成形”拍成一件物理事件：观众看着一整面画面从无序中长出来；反着用时，画面是被炸飞出画，而不是淡掉。 | image title | dramatic energetic cinematic | high | 6.3 beats |
| [`slam-drop`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/slam-drop/CARD.md) | Slam drop | The card lands with weight: the viewer feels it is heavy, and in the slot drop sees the force travel to its neighbours. | 卡片带着重量砸下：观众感到它很重；slot 式还能看见冲击力传到邻居身上。 | cards number | energetic dramatic | high | 2.4 beats |
| [`split-fuse`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/split-fuse/CARD.md) | Split fuse | Show what the thing is made of, then make it one: each part is recognised on its own before the slam makes them the whole. | 先让人看清它由什么组成，再合成一个：两半各自被认出来，扣合那一下让它们成为整体。 | image logo | dramatic energetic cinematic | peak | 10 beats |
| [`star-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/star-sweep/CARD.md) | Star sweep | Everything that was sent out comes back to one point the user can look at. | 派出去的一切都回到一个用户看得过来的点。 | abstract space | cinematic dramatic | peak | 8 beats |
| [`symbol-scramble`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/symbol-scramble/CARD.md) | Symbol scramble | The number feels measured rather than typed: it searches, then settles, and no frame shows a wrong value. | 让数字像是量出来的而不是写上去的：先搜索再落定，任何一帧都不显示错的数。 | number metric | mechanical technical | mid | 1 beats |
| [`tape-run`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tape-run/CARD.md) | Tape run | The size of the jump becomes distance and speed: the viewer feels how far the value went before reading the new number. | 跳变的大小变成路程和速度：观众先感到数值跑了多远，再读新数字。 | metric number | mechanical energetic | high | 7 beats |
| [`tilt-camera`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tilt-camera/CARD.md) | Tilt camera | The camera's pitch tells the story: a page lifted from the table into view, a pick from a desk of pages, or a dive from above onto the hero, each landing on one upright page. | 用俯仰角讲故事：像掀开桌上的图纸把一页抬到眼前，或在一桌页面里巡一圈挑中一张，或从高空俯冲落到主角上，最后都落在一张正立的页面。 | ui space | cinematic dramatic | high | 4.5 beats |
| [`time-freeze`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/time-freeze/CARD.md) | Time freeze | "This moment is worth stopping time for": the content freezes while we look at it from the side or mark it up, so one instant of a process lands with weight. | “这一刻值得停下时间看”：内容冻住，镜头绕到侧面看或在上面圈注，过程中的一瞬被郑重点出来。 | chart metric ui | dramatic cinematic | mid | 9.5 beats |
| [`tumble-stair`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/tumble-stair/CARD.md) | Tumble stair | A set of pages appears out of nothing and lines up with energy: cards flip in from their thin edges into a stair, showing "many capabilities" at a glance. | 一组页面从无到有、带着冲劲列队：卡片从薄边翻飞成阶梯，一眼看到“好多能力”。 | cards ui | energetic cinematic | high | 3.8 beats |
| [`unit-regroup`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/unit-regroup/CARD.md) | Unit regroup | Every dot is one real item, so the viewer can follow one dot from the crowd into its group and into the total, and believes the chart and the number are the same things. | 每个点就是一个真实的个体，观众能跟着某一颗从人群走进分组、再走进总数，于是相信图表和数字是同一批东西。 | chart number | technical energetic | high | 7.5 beats |
| [`width-jump`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/width-jump/CARD.md) | Width jump | The name moves with the music without moving at all: same place, same letters, a new width on every beat. | 名字跟着音乐动却没挪位置：同一位置、同样的字，每拍换一个宽度。 | logo title | energetic playful | mid | 5 beats |
| [`word-ignite`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/word-ignite/CARD.md) | Word ignite | Only the selling word gets the charge, so the viewer knows which word the line is for; outline nails it down in two landings, size then substance. | 整句里只有卖点词被“通电”，观众知道这句话为哪个词而说；outline 式用两次落定（先尺寸、后质感）把它钉死。 | title text | energetic dramatic | high | 4 beats |

| [`guide-letter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/guide-letter/CARD.md) | Guide lettering | Letters are drafted, not placed: guide lines first, then the word traced in reading order. | 字是画出来的，不是摆上去的：先打构造线，再按阅读顺序写出。 | title text | technical elegant | low | 3 beats |
| [`hand-mark`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/hand-mark/CARD.md) | Hand mark | Point like a person at the one thing that matters, so the viewer knows where to look. | 像人用手指一样指向那一件要紧的事，让观众知道看哪里。 | text abstract | warm editorial | low | 1.5 beats |
| [`isolate`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/isolate/CARD.md) | Isolate | "In the end, only this one thing": everything around one card goes dark as the camera pulls away, a quiet, heavy close on the point the film is for. | “最后只剩这一件事”：镜头后拉、周围逐张熄灭，安静而有分量地收在全片要说的那一点上。 | cards metric | dramatic calm | low | 7.5 beats |
| [`lead-word`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/lead-word/CARD.md) | Lead word | Reading order becomes physical order: the viewer is made to read the lead word alone first, then sees the sentence grow round it, and has read the subject twice. | 把阅读顺序做成物理顺序：观众被迫先单独读首词，再看整句围绕它长出来，落定时主词已经读了两遍。 | title logo | energetic cinematic | high | 3.5 beats |
| [`marker-stroke`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/marker-stroke/CARD.md) | Marker stroke | One hand-drawn stroke lifts a word out of the type, like someone circling the point on a poster, so the viewer knows which word is the claim. | 一笔手绘把一个词从排版里拎出来，像有人拿笔在海报上圈重点，观众知道主张落在哪个词上。 | title text | editorial warm playful | low | 1 beats |
| [`plate-slip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/plate-slip/CARD.md) | Plate slip | A hit in the language of print: the plates slip out of register and snap back, so the landing feels like ink stamped on paper, not a screen glitch. | 用印刷的语言打一拳：两块印版错位再“啪”地套准，命中像在纸上盖了一枚油墨章，而不是屏幕故障。 | title | editorial energetic playful | high | 4 beats |
| [`rank-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/rank-sweep/CARD.md) | Rank sweep | At the end of a chart everything but the protagonist steps back, so the viewer leaves with the one group the film is about. | 图表结尾，除主角外全部退后，观众带走的就是这支片讲的那一组。 | chart | calm ceremonial | low | 3 beats |
| [`sheen-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/sheen-sweep/CARD.md) | Sheen sweep | A silent crowning: one pass of light over the hero card says "this is the one" without decorating anything else. | 一次无声的加冕：一道光扫过主角卡一次，说“就是它”，别的都不装饰。 | cards | elegant calm | low | 2 beats |
| [`type-enter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-enter/CARD.md) | Type enter | The title arrives piece by piece in reading order, so the viewer reads it as it lands; blur stays out of the way, the other ways make the arrival an event. | 标题按阅读顺序一块块到位，观众边落边读；blur 不抢戏，其余几种把入场本身做成一件事。 | title text | calm elegant playful | mid | 3 beats |
| [`type-retype`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-retype/CARD.md) | Type and retype | A small drama of hesitate, reject, declare: the viewer sees the plain word thrown out and the real claim typed faster, so the claim reads as a decision. | 一出“犹豫、否定、宣言”的小戏：观众看着平庸的说法被删掉、真正的卖点被更快地打出来，主张读成一个决定。 | text title terminal | playful technical | mid | 4 beats |
| [`type-sync`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-sync/CARD.md) | Type sync | The text keeps time with the sound: fill leads the eye word by word with the voice, pump makes the beat visible in the strokes. | 文字跟着声音走：fill 随旁白一个词一个词牵着视线，pump 让鼓点在笔画上看得见。 | text title | energetic calm | mid | 6 beats |
| [`word-ignite`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/word-ignite/CARD.md) | Word ignite | Only the selling word gets the charge, so the viewer knows which word the line is for; outline nails it down in two landings, size then substance. | 整句里只有卖点词被“通电”，观众知道这句话为哪个词而说；outline 式用两次落定（先尺寸、后质感）把它钉死。 | title text | energetic dramatic | high | 4 beats |

#### Slot: rest

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`gradient-drift`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/gradient-drift/CARD.md) | Gradient drift | Keeps a still layout alive: a slow, ever-moving ground gives text-only passages a quiet pulse without pulling the eye. | 让静止排版“活着”：缓慢变化的底色给纯文字段落一层安静的生气，又不抢视线。 | abstract | calm elegant | low | 12 beats |
| [`outline-write`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/outline-write/CARD.md) | Outline write | Let a name feel composed rather than announced: one invisible hand writes every letter at once, so the word arrives as one calm act. | 让一个名字像被从容写成，而不是被喊出来：一只看不见的手同时写所有字，整个词作为一次安静的动作出现。 | title logo | calm elegant ceremonial | low | 4.4 beats |
| [`perspective-glide`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/perspective-glide/CARD.md) | Perspective glide | The page shows itself off: a long interface stands as a steep wall and slides past a still camera like train carriages, so it reads as a real object in space. | 页面在自我展示：长界面立成一堵斜墙，像列车车厢一样从不动的镜头前滑过，读成空间里的实物。 | ui space | cinematic technical | mid | 8 beats |
| [`rank-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/rank-sweep/CARD.md) | Rank sweep | At the end of a chart everything but the protagonist steps back, so the viewer leaves with the one group the film is about. | 图表结尾，除主角外全部退后，观众带走的就是这支片讲的那一组。 | chart | calm ceremonial | low | 3 beats |
| [`sheet-deal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/sheet-deal/CARD.md) | Sheet deal | Change the evidence without moving the camera: one sheet out, the next dealt on where the eye already is. | 不动镜头换证据：一张出去，下一张发到视线已经在的地方。 | image text | editorial calm | low | 1.5 beats |
| [`stagger-in`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/stagger-in/CARD.md) | Stagger in | Let a set be read in order while the screen stays alive; in the bars and dots sets the order itself becomes visible, as a gradient or as a wave of power coming on. | 让一组东西按顺序被读到，同时画面一直是活的；柱和点阵两式里，顺序本身也看得见：一道梯度，或一次从源头扩散开的通电。 | list chart abstract | calm technical | low | 8 beats |

#### Slot: lockup

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`brace-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/brace-open/CARD.md) | Brace open | The name is "bracketed out" as the content itself, so the viewer reads it as the thing being defined, with a developer's accent. | 名字被一对括号“括出来”，让观众把它读成被定义的那个东西本身，自带开发者语感。 | title logo code | technical elegant | mid | 5 beats |
| [`card-squash`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/card-squash/CARD.md) | Card squash | Turn the finished thing back into the line, so the close can grow from it. | 把做好的东西变回那条线，让收尾从线里长出来。 | cards abstract | calm cinematic | mid | 1 beats |
| [`caret-fall`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/caret-fall/CARD.md) | Caret falls flat | Keep one object on screen as it changes job, without crossing the words being read. | 让同一个物体换工作时一直在画面上，而且不越过正在读的字。 | cursor text | calm technical | low | 1 beats |
| [`gradient-drift`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/gradient-drift/CARD.md) | Gradient drift | Keeps a still layout alive: a slow, ever-moving ground gives text-only passages a quiet pulse without pulling the eye. | 让静止排版“活着”：缓慢变化的底色给纯文字段落一层安静的生气，又不抢视线。 | abstract | calm elegant | low | 12 beats |
| [`grain-condense`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/grain-condense/CARD.md) | Grain condense | A long line boils down to the one word that matters, so the viewer feels the message distilled into the name. | 一长句沸腾凝缩成最要紧的那个词，让观众感到信息被提纯成了名字。 | title logo | dramatic technical | high | 4 beats |
| [`isolate`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/isolate/CARD.md) | Isolate | "In the end, only this one thing": everything around one card goes dark as the camera pulls away, a quiet, heavy close on the point the film is for. | “最后只剩这一件事”：镜头后拉、周围逐张熄灭，安静而有分量地收在全片要说的那一点上。 | cards metric | dramatic calm | low | 7.5 beats |
| [`outline-write`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/outline-write/CARD.md) | Outline write | Let a name feel composed rather than announced: one invisible hand writes every letter at once, so the word arrives as one calm act. | 让一个名字像被从容写成，而不是被喊出来：一只看不见的手同时写所有字，整个词作为一次安静的动作出现。 | title logo | calm elegant ceremonial | low | 4.4 beats |
| [`pencil-loop`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/pencil-loop/CARD.md) | Pencil loop | A hand mark that reads as a choice, not as the system’s highlight. | 一道手迹，读起来是人的选择，而不是系统的高亮。 | image abstract | playful calm | mid | 2 beats |
| [`rank-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/rank-sweep/CARD.md) | Rank sweep | At the end of a chart everything but the protagonist steps back, so the viewer leaves with the one group the film is about. | 图表结尾，除主角外全部退后，观众带走的就是这支片讲的那一组。 | chart | calm ceremonial | low | 3 beats |
| [`ring-route`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/ring-route/CARD.md) | Ring route | Retrace the argument as one path through the evidence before the name. | 在名字出现前，把整条论证当作穿过证据的一条路线重走一遍。 | diagram abstract | calm elegant | low | 2 beats |
| [`seam-close`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/seam-close/CARD.md) | Seam close | End where the film began: the seam leaves, the caret waits. | 结束在开头的地方：接缝离开，光标等待。 | code title | calm ceremonial | low | 2 beats |
| [`shard-gather`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/shard-gather/CARD.md) | Shard gather | Film 'the product takes shape' as a physical event: a whole picture grows out of disorder before the viewer's eyes; reversed, it clears the screen by flying apart, not fading. | 把“产品成形”拍成一件物理事件：观众看着一整面画面从无序中长出来；反着用时，画面是被炸飞出画，而不是淡掉。 | image title | dramatic energetic cinematic | high | 6.3 beats |
| [`sheen-sweep`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/sheen-sweep/CARD.md) | Sheen sweep | A silent crowning: one pass of light over the hero card says "this is the one" without decorating anything else. | 一次无声的加冕：一道光扫过主角卡一次，说“就是它”，别的都不装饰。 | cards | elegant calm | low | 2 beats |
| [`split-fuse`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/split-fuse/CARD.md) | Split fuse | Show what the thing is made of, then make it one: each part is recognised on its own before the slam makes them the whole. | 先让人看清它由什么组成，再合成一个：两半各自被认出来，扣合那一下让它们成为整体。 | image logo | dramatic energetic cinematic | peak | 10 beats |
| [`type-enter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/type-enter/CARD.md) | Type enter | The title arrives piece by piece in reading order, so the viewer reads it as it lands; blur stays out of the way, the other ways make the arrival an event. | 标题按阅读顺序一块块到位，观众边落边读；blur 不抢戏，其余几种把入场本身做成一件事。 | title text | calm elegant playful | mid | 3 beats |
| [`width-jump`](../assets/runtime/bricks/motion/width-jump/CARD.md) | Width jump | The name moves with the music without moving at all: same place, same letters, a new width on every beat. | 名字跟着音乐动却没挪位置：同一位置、同样的字，每拍换一个宽度。 | logo title | energetic playful | mid | 5 beats |

### The runtime's transitions and hand-offs

Motions that ship in `assets/runtime/transitions/` and `assets/runtime/handoffs/`; their cards are in [transitions.md](transitions.md).

| Module | Family | Summary | Variants |
|---|---|---|---|
| [`anchor-swap`](transitions.md#anchor-swap) | zoom-depth | The anchor the eye is on stays put while the world around it is replaced; a final pull-back shows it is part of the mark. | – |
| [`aperture-swap`](transitions.md#aperture-swap) | zoom-depth | The word holds its place while an aperture (band, circle, closing bars or a cut) opens onto the next ground. | – |
| [`arc-curl`](transitions.md#arc-curl) | shape | A line of fixed length curls into a ring, or one outline morphs into the next while turning through its symmetry angle. | – |
| [`beam-trace`](transitions.md#beam-trace) | line-path | The old picture fades as phosphor afterglow; the beam returns and draws the new path, then the new reading settles. | – |
| [`bezel-pullback`](transitions.md#bezel-pullback) | zoom-depth | The asset fills the frame first; as the camera pulls back, its window grows outward from the asset's edges, titled with the source. | – |
| [`blueprint-expose`](transitions.md#blueprint-expose) | mask-type | A print lamp crosses the sheet; the new drawing develops behind it, line work first, then full colour. | – |
| [`camera-truck`](transitions.md#camera-truck) | horizontal | Assets lie side by side on one plane; the camera trucks about a screen to the next, blurred only while fast. | `travel` |
| [`collapse-burst`](transitions.md#collapse-burst) | shape | A word shrinks into its dot; the dot holds, squashes, bursts into particles that condense into the next word. | – |
| [`container-morph`](transitions.md#container-morph) | shape | One rounded rectangle springs from one interface state to the next; its content leaves first and enters after it lands. | – |
| [`converge-mark`](transitions.md#converge-mark) | shape | The film's pieces fly along arcs into the cells of the mark; a ring marks the empty cell and the dot lands in it last. | – |
| [`counter-portal`](transitions.md#counter-portal) | zoom-depth | The next scene waits inside a letter's counter; the camera pushes through the counter until it is the frame. | `stroke` |
| [`crt-collapse`](transitions.md#crt-collapse) | shape | The picture collapses like a CRT switched off (height, then width, to a dying spot) and the new one powers on. | – |
| [`device-flip`](transitions.md#device-flip) | lift-flip | A hidden cut inside one object, not a hand-off: the window turns edge-on, its content is swapped on the frame no face shows, and it turns back with the next page. | `over` |
| [`diff-slider`](transitions.md#diff-slider) | split-wipe | Two registered renders of one frame either side of the seam; the seam moves and the after takes over. | `scrub` |
| [`dither-sweep`](transitions.md#dither-sweep) | split-wipe | An ordered-dither front sweeps out from the origin; cells flick on in the phosphor accent, then show the new frame. | – |
| [`dot-iris`](transitions.md#dot-iris) | zoom-depth | The dot swells into a hard-edged disc that opens on the new frame (or the frame closes down to the dot). | `anchor` |
| [`feed-stack`](transitions.md#feed-stack) | vertical | The next screen slides up over the current one like the next post in a feed; the current one steps back into a deck. | – |
| [`field-morph`](transitions.md#field-morph) | shape | One halftone wave field changes parameters: a second source spreads, a front quantizes it, the blocks resolve into a sprite. | – |
| [`gate-punch`](transitions.md#gate-punch) | zoom-depth | A registered frame punches to the full frame, or the full frame shrinks into its place in the next scene. | – |
| [`grid-hop`](transitions.md#grid-hop) | zoom-depth | The frame pulls back into a grid of its siblings, a ring hops to the next tile, and the camera dives into it. | – |
| [`ink-drop`](transitions.md#ink-drop) | mask-type | A drop lands and blooms like ink in water; a rolling ring of ink opens the new frame inside it. | – |
| [`ink-wash`](transitions.md#ink-wash) | mask-type | Ink wicks through absorbent paper from a few brush touches; dark rims, uneven wash, the new frame where it settles. | `hair` |
| [`lift-out`](transitions.md#lift-out) | lift-flip | The ringed cells of a real asset rise in place and grow; their numbers are redrawn in code; the asset dims beneath (or the numbers land in the next scene). | – |
| [`louvre`](transitions.md#louvre) | split-wipe | The content is cut into slats (along a table's own rows); each slat pushes sideways in turn and brings in a slice of the next content. | `flip` |
| [`mark-to-chrome`](transitions.md#mark-to-chrome) | lift-flip | The official logo shrinks and flies to the top left to become a window's title; the window opens from there. | – |
| [`match-cut`](transitions.md#match-cut) | edit-cut | Push in on a value, cut on a still frame to the same value printed in the next source at the same place and size, pull back. | – |
| [`mosaic-assemble`](transitions.md#mosaic-assemble) | mask-type | The next asset assembles over the current one as a mosaic of tiles that grow from points, outward from the claim. | – |
| [`net-unfold`](transitions.md#net-unfold) | paper | A box opens along its edges into a flat cross; the six squares slide and stretch into the panels of a layout. | – |
| [`occluder-pass`](transitions.md#occluder-pass) | split-wipe | A defocused straightedge sweeps past the lens; the old frame becomes the new one entirely under its body. | `card` |
| [`parallax-slide`](transitions.md#parallax-slide) | horizontal | The camera slides to the next exhibit; numeral, window and caption chip move at three depths and land together. | `multiplane` |
| [`path-type`](transitions.md#path-type) | line-path | The line rolls up a loop and carries its sentence round and out; the next sentence follows it in and the loop opens. | – |
| [`plakat-columns`](transitions.md#plakat-columns) | split-wipe | An ink shutter runs up each of the twelve grid columns in turn; the new poster is behind it. | – |
| [`rail-press`](transitions.md#rail-press) | line-path | The line presses a word into itself, takes its weight, then grows into a bar that a blade cuts into the next word. | – |
| [`re-encode`](transitions.md#re-encode) | shape | Bar tops become points and the bars thin away; a trend curve then draws through the same points. | – |
| [`roll`](transitions.md#roll) | paper | The paper roll drops and the sheet hangs where it passed, or comes back up and winds the sheet onto itself. | – |
| [`roulette-lock`](transitions.md#roulette-lock) | horizontal | A frame jumps one candidate per half beat, laps the row, slows and locks on the answer; the others step back. | – |
| [`scramble-resolve`](transitions.md#scramble-resolve) | mask-type | A line resolves left to right; each cell flickers through the content's own alphabet until it locks. | `cycle` |
| [`scroll-fling`](transitions.md#scroll-fling) | vertical | The page flies back to its top: it accelerates, streaks past everything between and brakes softly onto the first section. | – |
| [`scroll-reel`](transitions.md#scroll-reel) | vertical | A long page is flicked through a still window, pulls back while fast, and coasts to an exact stop on the next section; headings pin as chapters. | – |
| [`section-lock`](transitions.md#section-lock) | vertical | Headings that reach the top stay pinned as the chapter while their section scrolls under them; the next one pushes them out. | – |
| [`shape-flood`](transitions.md#shape-flood) | mask-type | The subject's own outline grows from its centre and floods the frame; inside it, the same geometry in the next material. | – |
| [`sheet-pick`](transitions.md#sheet-pick) | zoom-depth | The cell the grease pencil ringed on the contact sheet punches to the full frame; the ring stays on the sheet. | – |
| [`sheet-shuffle`](transitions.md#sheet-shuffle) | paper | The top sheet is drawn out to the side and tucked under the pile; the sheet beneath squares up to be read. | – |
| [`signal-relay`](transitions.md#signal-relay) | line-path | A card collapses into its node, a pulse runs along the line (splitting into lanes), and the next nodes bloom into cards. | – |
| [`slice-smear`](transitions.md#slice-smear) | split-wipe | The frame is cut into text-line bands that slide off with streaks; the new frame's bands arrive as streaks that resolve. | – |
| [`slot-rails`](transitions.md#slot-rails) | vertical | A word leaves up through the top rail, the rails breathe to the next word, which rises in from the bottom rail. | – |
| [`split-branch`](transitions.md#split-branch) | split-wipe | The seam moves, then a branch runs down from it and opens a second result pane behind its tip. | – |
| [`split-open`](transitions.md#split-open) | split-wipe | A seam is cut down the content (or a caret stands up into it); the halves part like pocket doors, or the next content slides out of the seam. | `barn`, `slam` |
| [`spotlight-band`](transitions.md#spotlight-band) | mask-type | The asset dims to half; one hard-edged band stays lit and moves to the next region to read. | – |
| [`squash-to-path`](transitions.md#squash-to-path) | line-path | A word squashes into a line that becomes a looping path; a token runs it and the loops shrink until it is straight. | – |
| [`step-wipe`](transitions.md#step-wipe) | split-wipe | A staircase front whose steps are the next chart's values wipes across; the old bars retract into it; it stays as the chart. | – |
| [`stream-brake`](transitions.md#stream-brake) | vertical | A stream of lines runs past at speed, brakes exponentially and stops with the key item in the reading band. | `rows`, `overshoot` |
| [`strip-advance`](transitions.md#strip-advance) | horizontal | Frames on a strip of film move through a fixed gate, a pull-down at a time or in one run, and stop with the next frame registered. | – |
| [`stroke-reinterpret`](transitions.md#stroke-reinterpret) | shape | Three lines from the centre and three dashed hidden edges turn a flat hexagon into a cube without moving anything. | – |
| [`tally-flip`](transitions.md#tally-flip) | mask-type | The frame splits into unit tiles; each is marked in place, then turns over to the new frame, in order from the origin. | – |
| [`tether-link`](transitions.md#tether-link) | line-path | An accent line, led by a small dot, draws from a claim to its source in a real asset; a ring closes round the source. | `flyline` |
| [`thread-split`](transitions.md#thread-split) | split-wipe | One line draws down the frame and opens like a seam into curling marbled dye; the new frame is inside. | – |
| [`ticker-wipe`](transitions.md#ticker-wipe) | split-wipe | A ticker band drops down the frame wiping in the next asset; its crawl carries the next source in and stops as the caption. | – |
| [`token-sweep`](transitions.md#token-sweep) | split-wipe | The token bar’s cells grow into bars that sweep over the frame, then fall back to uncover the new one. | – |
| [`trace-flow`](transitions.md#trace-flow) | line-path | A front carried by a vector field sweeps across; content is dragged along the streamlines it lights up. | – |
| [`type-window`](transitions.md#type-window) | mask-type | The claim, set large in code, fills with its evidence through the letters, then the letters swell until the asset fills the frame. | – |
| [`unfold`](transitions.md#unfold) | vertical | A row opens in place: the page below slides down while what the row carries is revealed from the fold line. | – |
| [`value-carry`](transitions.md#value-carry) | lift-flip | A value lifts off one asset, rides a one-screen pan, and lands on the same value printed in a second asset. | – |
| [`viewport-push`](transitions.md#viewport-push) | zoom-depth | The window stays still; the asset inside pushes in on the region to read, never past its source resolution. | `overshoot`, `impact` |
| [`whip-push`](transitions.md#whip-push) | horizontal | The new card pushes the old one off with a strong motion streak along the push that is gone on landing. | `brake` |
| [`whiteout-slam`](transitions.md#whiteout-slam) | zoom-depth | The frame is exposed up to white, two white frames cut, and the answer lands with blur, scale and colour offset decaying to zero. | `inplace` |
| [`wipe-line`](transitions.md#wipe-line) | split-wipe | A copy of the seam crosses the frame; behind it the next state, ahead of it the current one. | – |

## Scene

### Seam (between two scenes)

#### Slot: hook

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### In-scene (inside one scene)

#### Slot: hook

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`anchor-ask`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/anchor-ask/CARD.md) | Anchor ask | The eye never moves: one word per beat on the same spot, the ground flipping under it, so the question lands as rhythm and the query under it says it is real. | 视线不用移动：同一位置一拍一个词，底色在下面翻，问题变成节奏；下方的查询行说明它是真的。 | title text code | energetic technical | high | 2–12 beats |
| [`anchor-rise`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/anchor-rise/CARD.md) | Anchor rise | The one brand piece arrives first and the problem lands beside it, so the viewer has a focal point before any feature. | 唯一的品牌件先到，问题落在它旁边，观众在任何功能之前就有了视觉焦点。 | title logo abstract | editorial dramatic | high | 4–8 beats |
| [`beat-list`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/beat-list/CARD.md) | Beat list | A run of names felt rather than read: one word a beat, each catching the lens and retinting the whole frame, so the list becomes a metronome. | 一串名字用来“感受”而不是读：每拍一个词进取景框，整屏跟着换色调，清单成了节拍器。 | list title | energetic playful | mid | 6–12 beats |
| [`blueprint-build`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/blueprint-build/CARD.md) | Blueprint build | The page builds itself in seconds, so the viewer feels the product generate the result, not draw it by hand. | 页面几秒内自己长出来，让观众感到结果是产品生成的，不是手画的。 | ui diagram | technical energetic | high | 11–13 beats |
| [`brief-loop`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/brief-loop/CARD.md) | Brief round the loop | Start from the one sentence the tool is given, typed by the line that will make everything else. | 从交给工具的那一句话开始，由那条之后会变成一切的线敲出来。 | text cursor | calm playful | mid | 6–14 beats |
| [`card-pile`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/card-pile/CARD.md) | Card pile | Say 'there is a whole set of these' in one image: the pile gives weight (how many), then either the fan shows they differ and picks one, or the stream shows a steady throughput being read. | 一个画面讲清“有一整套这种东西”：先用一摞给出分量（数量感），再要么展成扇面看出各不相同并抽出主角，要么源源不断压上来，读出持续的吞吐量。 | cards | energetic editorial | mid | 8–12 beats |
| [`chip-split`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/chip-split/CARD.md) | Chip split | The input stops being words and becomes units the run will act on, so the viewer knows what is being counted from the first seconds. | 输入从“词”变成运行要处理的单元，观众开头几秒就知道数的是什么。 | diagram code | technical calm | mid | 4–8 beats |
| [`crane-reveal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/crane-reveal/CARD.md) | Crane reveal | Start on one concrete item, then widen to the system around it, so the viewer understands 'the row you just saw is one cell of a whole wall, and it is done'. | 从一个具体条目起，再拉开到它所在的整个系统，观众读出“刚才那一行，只是这一整面墙的一格，而且已经完成了”。 | list ui metric | cinematic technical | mid | 9–12 beats |
| [`desk-threaded`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/desk-threaded/CARD.md) | Desk threaded | Start from the overload everyone recognises, then let one thread take all of it in one move. | 从人人认得的“太多了”开始，然后让一根线一下子全部接走。 | ui cursor abstract | dramatic energetic | high | 24–44 beats |
| [`dot-slam`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/dot-slam/CARD.md) | Dot slam | A statement lands like a stamp, and the full stop makes it final. | 一句陈述像盖章一样落下，句号让它成为定论。 | title number | energetic dramatic | high | 2–8 beats |
| [`exhibit-strike`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/exhibit-strike/CARD.md) | Exhibit strike | Show the claim where it was printed and strike it, so the viewer sees the evidence before the words. | 在印出断言的原处把它划出来，让观众先看到证据再看到话。 | image chart metric | editorial technical | mid | 4–10 beats |
| [`flow-landscape`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/flow-landscape/CARD.md) | Flow landscape | Set a world before naming anything: countless streams of work flowing into one trunk, seen from a low camera gliding over them, so the product is felt as what all that work becomes. | 在说出任何名字之前先立一个世界：无数工作流汇进一条主干，从低空缓缓飞越，观众先感到“这么多东西汇成了一个产品”。 | space abstract diagram | cinematic calm | mid | 12–14 beats |
| [`frame-forerun`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/frame-forerun/CARD.md) | Frame forerun | Give one panel a ceremony of arrival: the frame claims the place first, then the page wakes inside it and its parts are set down from the air, so the viewer sees the interface being placed, not appearing. | 给一块面板一场登场仪式：框先到、占住位置，页面在框里亮起，部件从空中带影贴落，观众看到的是界面被“安放”进去，而不是凭空出现。 | ui device | dramatic cinematic energetic | high | 8–11 beats |
| [`graze-tour`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/graze-tour/CARD.md) | Graze tour | The interface as a landscape: the camera skims its surface and the page completes itself as it passes, so the viewer feels inside the product's world. | 把界面拍成地形：镜头贴着表面掠过，页面在镜头经过时自己完成，观众像身在产品的内里世界。 | ui space | cinematic technical | mid | 10–10 beats |
| [`hero-spotlight`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/hero-spotlight/CARD.md) | Hero spotlight | Make one object the protagonist of the film: of everything on the page, this card; the lamp chooses it, the lift gives it weight, the running light inspects it, and the reseat says it belongs to the page. | 把一个对象立成全片主角：页面上那么多，偏偏是这一张；聚光替观众选中它，弹起给它分量，轮廓光检视它，贴回原位说明它属于这个页面。 | cards ui | cinematic dramatic elegant | mid | 11.5–11.5 beats |
| [`icon-field`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/icon-field/CARD.md) | Icon field | Lay out the whole universe of features in grey so the viewer registers 'this many', then light it all in one fast sweep, so the product reads as what brings it all to life. | 先用灰阶把整个功能宇宙摆满，让观众意识到“有这么多”，再用几道快速光带一举点亮，产品就是让这一切亮起来的那一个。 | abstract logo | ceremonial energetic | mid | 10–12 beats |
| [`layer-overview`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/layer-overview/CARD.md) | Layer overview | Give the viewer the whole map first, so every detail later has a place on it. | 先给观众整张地图，之后每个细节都在上面有位置。 | diagram | calm technical | low | 8–32 beats |
| [`name-stamp`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/name-stamp/CARD.md) | Name stamp | Give the viewer the name to remember before anything of the product is shown: quiet, pressed like ink, with one complete still moment. | 在任何产品画面出现之前先让观众看清并记住名字：安静、像墨一样压印，有一个完整的静止时刻。 | title logo | calm editorial ceremonial | low | 5–9 beats |
| [`orbit-title`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/orbit-title/CARD.md) | Orbit title | Hold up one claim with a whole set of real product pictures circling it: cards always entering and leaving say 'there is more', while the title stays fixed in the middle. | 用一整圈真实产品画面托住一句主张：卡片永远有进有出，暗示“还有更多”，标题稳稳钉在正中。 | title cards image | editorial technical | mid | 6–12 beats |
| [`pill-dictate`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/pill-dictate/CARD.md) | Pill dictate | A rest between louder beats: the input glows while it waits for you, and gives its light to the words as they arrive, so the request feels calm and personal. | 高能段之间的一个休止：输入条等你开口时亮着光，字一出来就把光让给文字，请求显得安静而亲近。 | voice ui text | calm warm elegant | low | 5–9 beats |
| [`point-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/point-line/CARD.md) | Point to line | A film opens on the smallest thing, a point, and promotes it into the first fact, so the viewer is inside the product's logic from frame one. | 片子从最小的东西——一个点——开场，把它升级成第一条事实，观众从第一帧起就在产品的逻辑里。 | code abstract | elegant technical | mid | 5–9 beats |
| [`print-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/print-open/CARD.md) | Print open | Start inside the evidence, then step back to show it is a print on a desk with a source. | 从证据内里开场，再退后，让人看到它是桌上一张有出处的截图。 | image chart ui | calm editorial | mid | 5–12 beats |
| [`problem-table`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/problem-table/CARD.md) | Problem table | The problem is stated large, then shown to be concrete: each broken fact gets its own row and an ink flag, so the claim that follows answers something real. | 先把问题说大，再证明它是具体的：每个坏掉的事实占一行、盖一枚墨标，后面的主张就是在回答真问题。 | title list | editorial dramatic | mid | 8–24 beats |
| [`prompt-boot`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/prompt-boot/CARD.md) | Prompt boot | Open in the terminal with the failure itself, then turn the whole screen into the point the film grows from. | 在终端里直接以失败开场，再把整个屏幕收成一个光点，片子从这里长出来。 | terminal code title | dramatic technical | high | 10–20 beats |
| [`reel-titles`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/reel-titles/CARD.md) | Reel titles | Words arrive as frames of the film itself, one pull-down at a time. | 文字作为胶片本身的画格到来，一次拉一格。 | title text | mechanical calm | mid | 2–16 beats |
| [`request-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/request-open/CARD.md) | Request open | The edit point becomes the boundary: what is typed opens onto what it makes. | 编辑点变成边界：打下的字通向它做出的东西。 | code image title | calm technical | mid | 5–14 beats |
| [`review-callouts`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/review-callouts/CARD.md) | Review callouts | State the problem the way a reviewer would: as open items on the drawing, each flagged by a person. | 像评审人那样陈述问题：画在图上的未决项，每项都被人标出。 | title list diagram | technical editorial | mid | 7–14 beats |
| [`screen-wake`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/screen-wake/CARD.md) | Screen wake | The film opens on the product’s own surface coming to life, so everything after reads as the real thing. | 片子从产品自己的画面亮起开场，之后的一切都读作真东西。 | device ui title | calm ceremonial | mid | 4–12 beats |
| [`sheet-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/sheet-open/CARD.md) | Sheet open | Open the page itself: the viewer sees the real thing hang and the first line read out. | 直接打开页面本身：观众看到真东西挂下来，第一句被读出来。 | text image | editorial calm | mid | 6–12 beats |
| [`spine-intro`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/spine-intro/CARD.md) | Spine intro | Let the product speak for itself, from the thread that just took everything. | 让产品亲口说话，从刚接走一切的那根线上。 | ui logo text | calm warm | mid | 12–26 beats |
| [`term-count`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/term-count/CARD.md) | Terminal count | Hook the viewer with a plain question and a number big enough to need explaining. | 用一个朴素的问题和一个大到需要解释的数字抓住观众。 | terminal number | calm technical | low | 10–40 beats |
| [`unit-count`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/unit-count/CARD.md) | Unit count | The viewer feels the size of the set before any category: a number counts while one square per item fills the frame. | 观众先感到集合有多大，再谈分类：数字往上数，同时一件一格铺满画面。 | number chart | calm technical | mid | 4–10 beats |
| [`voice-listen`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/voice-listen/CARD.md) | Voice listen | A live receipt that it is listening: the viewer reads what was said in the transcript and sees in the waveform that it was heard, rising when you speak and flattening when you pause, then the words are sent. | “它真的在听”的实时回执：观众从转写读到说了什么，从声纹看出被听见了（说话时高耸、停顿时塌平），最后这句话被发出去。 | voice ui | technical warm | mid | 9–14 beats |
| [`weight-word`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/weight-word/CARD.md) | Weight swing word | Answer the brief in one word and make the beat visible in its weight. | 用一个词回答需求，让节拍在字重上看得见。 | title | energetic playful | high | 2–6 beats |
| [`word-swap`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-swap/CARD.md) | Word swap | Each word is a new page: the ground changes with the word, opened by the dot, so three words read as three hits. | 每个词都是新的一页：底色随词换，由墨点打开，三个词读起来像三下重击。 | title text | energetic dramatic | high | 3–12 beats |
| [`word-tunnel`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-tunnel/CARD.md) | Word tunnel | Say 'a lot of things, all here' without asking anyone to read them: the product's nouns stream out of the depth past the camera, so the viewer reads three or five at a time but feels there are many, behind one still title. | 不要求观众读，也能说清“很多东西，全在这里”：产品的名词从纵深飞向镜头并掠过，观众每次只读到三五个，却感到很多，前面压着一行静止的标题。 | text title space | energetic cinematic | high | 8–16 beats |
| [`word-window`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-window/CARD.md) | Word window | Make the name and the product one picture: the word is a door cut out of the stage, the product is seen through it, and as the word opens the viewer walks from the name into the product. | 让名字和产品成为同一个画面：字是在舞台上挖出的门，产品透过笔画显形，字胀开时观众从品牌词直接走进产品。 | title ui image | dramatic cinematic energetic | high | 11–11 beats |

#### Slot: problem

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`anchor-ask`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/anchor-ask/CARD.md) | Anchor ask | The eye never moves: one word per beat on the same spot, the ground flipping under it, so the question lands as rhythm and the query under it says it is real. | 视线不用移动：同一位置一拍一个词，底色在下面翻，问题变成节奏；下方的查询行说明它是真的。 | title text code | energetic technical | high | 2–12 beats |
| [`anchor-rise`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/anchor-rise/CARD.md) | Anchor rise | The one brand piece arrives first and the problem lands beside it, so the viewer has a focal point before any feature. | 唯一的品牌件先到，问题落在它旁边，观众在任何功能之前就有了视觉焦点。 | title logo abstract | editorial dramatic | high | 4–8 beats |
| [`cell-count`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/cell-count/CARD.md) | Cell count | A small count is felt as things appearing: one cell per step round the anchor while the number climbs. | 小计数被感受为东西一件件出现：数字往上走，锚点周围每一步多一个细胞。 | number abstract | editorial dramatic | mid | 4–8 beats |
| [`claim-shift`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/claim-shift/CARD.md) | Claim shift | Keep the page and move the eye: the next claim is a step down the same evidence. | 页面不动，视线移动：下一条断言就在同一份证据里往下一步。 | image chart metric | editorial calm | low | 3–8 beats |
| [`desk-threaded`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/desk-threaded/CARD.md) | Desk threaded | Start from the overload everyone recognises, then let one thread take all of it in one move. | 从人人认得的“太多了”开始，然后让一根线一下子全部接走。 | ui cursor abstract | dramatic energetic | high | 24–44 beats |
| [`dot-slam`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/dot-slam/CARD.md) | Dot slam | A statement lands like a stamp, and the full stop makes it final. | 一句陈述像盖章一样落下，句号让它成为定论。 | title number | energetic dramatic | high | 2–8 beats |
| [`exhibit-strike`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/exhibit-strike/CARD.md) | Exhibit strike | Show the claim where it was printed and strike it, so the viewer sees the evidence before the words. | 在印出断言的原处把它划出来，让观众先看到证据再看到话。 | image chart metric | editorial technical | mid | 4–10 beats |
| [`field-card`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/field-card/CARD.md) | Field card | Each feature is acted out by the picture: the field round the anchor changes its function to show what the word means. | 每个功能由画面演出来：锚点周围的场改变自己的函数，表现这个词的意思。 | title abstract | editorial energetic | mid | 3–8 beats |
| [`figure-rows`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/figure-rows/CARD.md) | Figure rows | Each figure is a fact that arrives on the beat and stays still to be read; the scramble says it was measured, not typed. | 每个数字是一条在拍子上到达、然后停住让人读的事实；乱码定格说明它是量出来的，不是写上去的。 | number metric | energetic technical | mid | 3–12 beats |
| [`layer-overview`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/layer-overview/CARD.md) | Layer overview | Give the viewer the whole map first, so every detail later has a place on it. | 先给观众整张地图，之后每个细节都在上面有位置。 | diagram | calm technical | low | 8–32 beats |
| [`problem-table`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/problem-table/CARD.md) | Problem table | The problem is stated large, then shown to be concrete: each broken fact gets its own row and an ink flag, so the claim that follows answers something real. | 先把问题说大，再证明它是具体的：每个坏掉的事实占一行、盖一枚墨标，后面的主张就是在回答真问题。 | title list | editorial dramatic | mid | 8–24 beats |
| [`quote-detent`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/quote-detent/CARD.md) | Quote detent | Read the page's claims one at a time, each marked where it is written and said in large type. | 一次读一句页面上的主张：在原处划线，再用大字说出来。 | text image | editorial calm | mid | 4–12 beats |
| [`review-callouts`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/review-callouts/CARD.md) | Review callouts | State the problem the way a reviewer would: as open items on the drawing, each flagged by a person. | 像评审人那样陈述问题：画在图上的未决项，每项都被人标出。 | title list diagram | technical editorial | mid | 7–14 beats |
| [`scan-read`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/scan-read/CARD.md) | Scan read | The viewer watches the product read a page and name what it finds, so its understanding feels concrete and fast. | 看着产品读一页内容并逐块说出读到了什么，让它的“理解”具体又迅速。 | ui text | technical mechanical | mid | 10–12 beats |
| [`session-queue`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/session-queue/CARD.md) | Session queue | Reverse the viewer's belief: the person thought they were directing the agents; the picture shows the agents pulling the person. | 翻转观众的看法：以为是人在指挥 agent；画面里是 agent 在拽人。 | terminal ui | playful warm | mid | 16–48 beats |
| [`term-count`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/term-count/CARD.md) | Terminal count | Hook the viewer with a plain question and a number big enough to need explaining. | 用一个朴素的问题和一个大到需要解释的数字抓住观众。 | terminal number | calm technical | low | 10–40 beats |
| [`unit-count`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/unit-count/CARD.md) | Unit count | The viewer feels the size of the set before any category: a number counts while one square per item fills the frame. | 观众先感到集合有多大，再谈分类：数字往上数，同时一件一格铺满画面。 | number chart | calm technical | mid | 4–10 beats |
| [`versus`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/versus/CARD.md) | Versus | A comparison settled in one hit: the old way and the new slam together, each a number, so the gap is felt as a clash rather than read in a table. | 一击定胜负的对比：老办法和新办法对撞合拢，各带一个数字，差距是“撞”出来的，而不是从表格里读出来的。 | number text | dramatic energetic | high | 6–10 beats |
| [`wall-ledger`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/wall-ledger/CARD.md) | Wall to ledger | Show where the number comes from: a machine read every item and each one became a dot on the page. | 交代数字从哪来：机器读了每一件，每件都成了纸上一颗点。 | terminal number list | technical calm | mid | 8–24 beats |
| [`weight-histogram`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/weight-histogram/CARD.md) | Weight histogram | Counting that is weighted is drawn: each row's weight flies to its bar, so the viewer sees why the top pair wins. | 加权计数被画出来：每行的权重飞进自己的条，观众看到为什么最上面那对赢。 | chart diagram | technical | mid | 4–10 beats |
| [`word-warp`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-warp/CARD.md) | Word warp | Instability made visible: the problem word cannot hold its shape while the plain word under it lands hard. | 让不稳定看得见：问题词保持不住形状，下面那个平实的词却重重落下。 | title text | energetic playful | high | 4–8 beats |
