| [`doc-findings`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/doc-findings/CARD.md) | Doc findings | Give conclusions one at a time without losing their source: the document stays on screen and is still being read, and every finding is visibly pulled out of one of its lines. | 一条条给出结论又不丢出处：文档留在画面里、还在被读，每条结论都看得出是从它的某一行里长出来的。 | text list | calm editorial | low | 10–14 beats |
| [`dot-burst`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/dot-burst/CARD.md) | Dot burst | The method is born out of the guess: the same ink, rearranged. | 方法从猜测里生出来：同一滴墨，重新排列。 | title text | dramatic energetic | high | 4–8 beats |
| [`field-card`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/field-card/CARD.md) | Field card | Each feature is acted out by the picture: the field round the anchor changes its function to show what the word means. | 每个功能由画面演出来：锚点周围的场改变自己的函数，表现这个词的意思。 | title abstract | editorial energetic | mid | 3–8 beats |
| [`film-ruler`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/film-ruler/CARD.md) | Film ruler | The film measures itself: the dot is exactly where the film is, so "half way" is literally true. | 片子自己量自己：墨点正好在片子走到的位置，“过半”是字面上的真。 | timeline title | mechanical technical | high | 4–8 beats |
| [`frame-forerun`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/frame-forerun/CARD.md) | Frame forerun | Give one panel a ceremony of arrival: the frame claims the place first, then the page wakes inside it and its parts are set down from the air, so the viewer sees the interface being placed, not appearing. | 给一块面板一场登场仪式：框先到、占住位置，页面在框里亮起，部件从空中带影贴落，观众看到的是界面被“安放”进去，而不是凭空出现。 | ui device | dramatic cinematic energetic | high | 8–11 beats |
| [`general-arrangement`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/general-arrangement/CARD.md) | General arrangement | Show the product as one designed part between an input and an output, dimensioned like a real assembly. | 把产品画成输入与输出之间的一个被设计的部件，像真正的装配图一样标尺寸。 | diagram title | technical calm | mid | 7–14 beats |
| [`hex-solid`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/hex-solid/CARD.md) | Hexagon to cube | A flat thing is revealed as a solid source, so the viewer understands that everything else is generated from it. | 平面的东西被揭示为一个立体的源头，观众明白其余一切都是从它生成的。 | abstract diagram | elegant dramatic | high | 6–10 beats |
| [`hop-search`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/hop-search/CARD.md) | Hop search | The viewer watches the answer being found: anticipation builds hop by hop and resolves on the lock, or the failure is stated plainly. | 观众看着答案被找出来：一格一格跳，期待越来越强，锁定时落地；或者干脆说明一个都没过。 | list metric number | energetic mechanical | high | 4–12 beats |
| [`hub-links`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/hub-links/CARD.md) | Hub links | Turn 'many sources, one place' into a physical process: paths connect, and the sources are either swallowed so only the product remains (merge) or stay and keep feeding it (connect). | 把“多个来源、一个去处”变成看得见的物理过程：通路接上，来源要么被吸进去、最后只剩产品（汇流），要么留在原处持续输送（接入）。 | diagram logo | technical calm | mid | 6–12 beats |
| [`input-output`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/input-output/CARD.md) | Input and output | The claim is a transformation the eye can check at a glance: the person's part is in the colour, the product's result in ink, and the arrow is the product. | 主张是一次一眼能核对的转换：人的部分在色块里，产品的结果是墨色，箭头就是产品本身。 | title text | editorial dramatic | high | 8–18 beats |
| [`milestone-run`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/milestone-run/CARD.md) | Milestone run | Tell a history as speed: past versions flash by like scenery, and the hard stop on today says 'this is what matters now'. | 把一段历史讲成速度：过去的版本像窗外风景一样闪过，在“今天”急刹，说明“现在这个最重要”。 | timeline cards | energetic cinematic | high | 8–12 beats |
| [`numeral-chapter`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/numeral-chapter/CARD.md) | Numeral chapter | The film announces its structure like a poster series: a giant numeral says where you are before the page says what. | 片子像一套海报那样报出结构：先用巨型数字说“第几部分”，再由页面说“是什么”。 | number title | editorial ceremonial | mid | 3–12 beats |
| [`option-pick`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/option-pick/CARD.md) | Option pick | Show that a person decides, and what: the accent fills the chosen option, so the viewer sees each decision being made. | 让人看到“是人在决定、决定了什么”：强调色填进被选的那项，每个决定都看得见。 | list ui | editorial calm | mid | 8–20 beats |
| [`pile-split`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/pile-split/CARD.md) | Pile split | Let the viewer feel the proportion first, then read the figure, then see the part that still needs a person. | 先让观众感到比例，再读到数字，再看到其中仍需要人的那一部分。 | number list | calm editorial | mid | 10–40 beats |
| [`qa-pick`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/qa-pick/CARD.md) | Questions and picks | Show that the person decides: the tool asks, the person picks, on the beat. | 表明做决定的是人：工具提问，人在拍点上选择。 | terminal ui list | technical calm | mid | 6–20 beats |
| [`quote-detent`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/quote-detent/CARD.md) | Quote detent | Read the page's claims one at a time, each marked where it is written and said in large type. | 一次读一句页面上的主张：在原处划线，再用大字说出来。 | text image | editorial calm | mid | 4–12 beats |
| [`screen-change`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/screen-change/CARD.md) | Screen change | The device stays the through-line while the evidence changes under the glass. | 设备始终是贯穿线索，证据在玻璃下更换。 | device ui image | calm technical | mid | 3–12 beats |
| [`space-steps`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/space-steps/CARD.md) | Space steps | Three steps told as three places in one space: each chapter gets its own angle, and the pull-back at the end shows how they make one whole. | 把三步讲成同一空间里的三个地方：每章换一个空间视角，最后拉远让观众看到三者合成一个整体。 | cards space title | cinematic elegant | mid | 15–15 beats |
| [`spine-intro`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/spine-intro/CARD.md) | Spine intro | Let the product speak for itself, from the thread that just took everything. | 让产品亲口说话，从刚接走一切的那根线上。 | ui logo text | calm warm | mid | 12–26 beats |
| [`unit-axis`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/unit-axis/CARD.md) | Unit axis | Overlap becomes a surprise you can see: the subset shrinks into an axis and bars grow out of it, so one item naming several things is shown, not claimed. | 重叠变成看得见的意外：子集缩成一根轴，条从轴上长出来，一件东西同时属于几类是画出来的，不是说出来的。 | chart | dramatic technical | high | 6–14 beats |
| [`unit-periods`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/unit-periods/CARD.md) | Unit periods | Time becomes columns of the same items, and the protagonist at the base of every column lights together, so 'when' and 'is it rising again' read in one picture. | 时间变成同一批方块堆成的柱，每根柱底部的主角一起亮起，“什么时候”和“是否又在上升”一眼读出。 | chart | technical editorial | mid | 4–14 beats |
| [`value-match`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/value-match/CARD.md) | Value match | Prove two sources agree by cutting between them on the very value they share. | 在两份来源共有的那个值上切换，证明它们一致。 | number image chart | dramatic technical | high | 5–12 beats |

| [`word-swap`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-swap/CARD.md) | Word swap | Each word is a new page: the ground changes with the word, opened by the dot, so three words read as three hits. | 每个词都是新的一页：底色随词换，由墨点打开，三个词读起来像三下重击。 | title text | energetic dramatic | high | 3–12 beats |

| [`bento-wall`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/bento-wall/CARD.md) | Bento wall | Show that a whole set of capabilities is already there and simply switches on, so the viewer reads a calm, complete overview instead of things pouring in. | 表现一整面功能本来就在原位、只是被逐一点亮，观众读到的是一份沉稳、完整的总览，而不是一堆东西涌进来。 | cards ui | ceremonial calm technical | mid | 7–11 beats |

| [`card-pile`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/card-pile/CARD.md) | Card pile | Say 'there is a whole set of these' in one image: the pile gives weight (how many), then either the fan shows they differ and picks one, or the stream shows a steady throughput being read. | 一个画面讲清“有一整套这种东西”：先用一摞给出分量（数量感），再要么展成扇面看出各不相同并抽出主角，要么源源不断压上来，读出持续的吞吐量。 | cards | energetic editorial | mid | 8–12 beats |

| [`cursor-crowd`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/cursor-crowd/CARD.md) | Cursor crowd | Make the team's presence felt: named cursors arriving and drifting give the board the warmth of people in the room, and one of them typing the brief turns presence into work done together. | 让人感到团队在场：具名光标飞入、漂移，给白板铺上“有人在”的体温；其中一枚写出简报标题，把在场变成一起干活。 | cursor ui cards | playful warm | mid | 8–12 beats |
| [`cursor-tour`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/cursor-tour/CARD.md) | Cursor tour | One continuous camera walks the viewer through four features of one page; the cursor is their pointing finger, and each click gets an answer, so cause and place are both remembered. | 一个连续镜头带观众走完一页上的四个功能，光标是导游的手指，每点一下页面就回应，既记住因果也记住位置。 | ui cursor | technical calm | mid | 14–16 beats |

| [`deck-deal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/deck-deal/CARD.md) | Deck deal | Build the first impression of volume with suspense: a pile raises 'what is it?', then dozens of items fly into place faster and faster, so the viewer feels content pouring in. | 带着悬念建立“量很大”的第一印象：先是一摞牌（这是什么？），再是几十项越甩越快地飞进页面各就各位，观众感到东西源源不断涌进来。 | cards ui | energetic cinematic | high | 9–11 beats |

| [`evidence-relay`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/evidence-relay/CARD.md) | Evidence relay | Pair each claim with its proof: the verb on the right is the argument, the page card on the left is the evidence, and the column steps only when the word changes, so text and picture footnote each other. | 让每个说法都配上证据：右边的动词是论点，左边的页面卡是证据，只在换词时步进一格，文字和画面互为注脚。 | title image ui | editorial calm | mid | 9–18 beats |

| [`scroll-wall`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/scroll-wall/CARD.md) | Scroll wall | Say 'there is far more than we can show you' in a few seconds: a standing wall of pages keeps flowing past, read as volume, never as detail. | 几秒钟里说清“多到给你看不完”：一面页面墙持续流过，读到的是体量，不是细节。 | image ui cards | calm technical | mid | 8–12 beats |

| [`tree-cascade`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/tree-cascade/CARD.md) | Tree cascade | Show a structure being born from one sentence: links pull nodes out level by level, so the viewer reads 'it builds the whole structure from what I asked', with the order of growth carrying the sense of structure. | 表现一个结构从一句话里诞生：连线逐层把节点拉出来，观众读出“它从我这句话搭出了整个结构”，结构感来自生长的先后。 | diagram ui text | technical calm | mid | 10–14 beats |

| [`answer-whiteout`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/answer-whiteout/CARD.md) | Answer whiteout | The peak is marked by being the only flash: the answer arrives on the hit, in the one colour that means "answer", and is given time to sink in. | 高潮靠“唯一一次闪白”来标记：答案踩着重拍到来，用唯一代表“答案”的颜色，并留出时间让人消化。 | number title | dramatic energetic | peak | 4–10 beats |

| [`emblem-relay`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/emblem-relay/CARD.md) | Emblem relay | Let each benefit word be argued by its own geometry, spinning rings for quick, locking rings for tight, a metal sheen for the final word, so three words become one continuous claim rather than three title cards. | 让每个利益词由专属几何替它论证：转动的环是“快”、咬合的环是“紧”、金属光泽给最后那个结论词，三个词连成一句连贯的主张，而不是三张字卡。 | title abstract | energetic ceremonial | high | 8–16 beats |
| [`exhibit-ledger`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/exhibit-ledger/CARD.md) | Exhibit ledger | Close the file: the viewer sees every claim again, each tied to its source. | 合上卷宗：观众再看一遍每条断言，各自连着出处。 | list text | editorial calm | low | 4–12 beats |
| [`exploded-chain`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/exploded-chain/CARD.md) | Exploded chain | Assemble the system in front of the viewer: separate parts click into one chain, and the people at its ends are amber. | 在观众眼前把系统装起来：分散的部件咔哒合成一条链，两端的人是琥珀色。 | diagram | mechanical technical | mid | 7–16 beats |
| [`flow-landscape`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/flow-landscape/CARD.md) | Flow landscape | Set a world before naming anything: countless streams of work flowing into one trunk, seen from a low camera gliding over them, so the product is felt as what all that work becomes. | 在说出任何名字之前先立一个世界：无数工作流汇进一条主干，从低空缓缓飞越，观众先感到“这么多东西汇成了一个产品”。 | space abstract diagram | cinematic calm | mid | 12–14 beats |
| [`gap-measure`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/gap-measure/CARD.md) | Gap measure | The difference is drawn on the evidence and computed, so the claim cannot be argued with. | 差距画在证据上、由计算得出，主张因此无可辩驳。 | chart metric number | dramatic technical | high | 4–12 beats |
| [`general-arrangement`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/general-arrangement/CARD.md) | General arrangement | Show the product as one designed part between an input and an output, dimensioned like a real assembly. | 把产品画成输入与输出之间的一个被设计的部件，像真正的装配图一样标尺寸。 | diagram title | technical calm | mid | 7–14 beats |
| [`horizon-hero`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/horizon-hero/CARD.md) | Horizon hero | Make the claim the horizon of the film: one line of light, the product rising from it, the promise written under it. | 把主张做成整部片子的地平线：一条光线，产品从中升起，承诺写在线下。 | title space abstract | cinematic elegant | mid | 5–14 beats |
| [`ink-words`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/ink-words/CARD.md) | Ink and words | Say what the output is in a few words over something only code could draw. | 在只有代码画得出的东西上，用几个词说出产出是什么。 | abstract text | cinematic calm | mid | 4–8 beats |
| [`input-output`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/input-output/CARD.md) | Input and output | The claim is a transformation the eye can check at a glance: the person's part is in the colour, the product's result in ink, and the arrow is the product. | 主张是一次一眼能核对的转换：人的部分在色块里，产品的结果是墨色，箭头就是产品本身。 | title text | editorial dramatic | high | 8–18 beats |
| [`layer-overview`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/layer-overview/CARD.md) | Layer overview | Give the viewer the whole map first, so every detail later has a place on it. | 先给观众整张地图，之后每个细节都在上面有位置。 | diagram | calm technical | low | 8–32 beats |
| [`neon-marquee`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/neon-marquee/CARD.md) | Neon marquee | Three words the film earned glow like signs on a street at night: a recap with a party mood before the end. | 片子挣来的三个词像夜街招牌一样亮起：带点派对气氛的回顾，收尾前喘口气。 | title space | energetic cinematic | mid | 10–10 beats |
| [`orbit-title`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/orbit-title/CARD.md) | Orbit title | Hold up one claim with a whole set of real product pictures circling it: cards always entering and leaving say 'there is more', while the title stays fixed in the middle. | 用一整圈真实产品画面托住一句主张：卡片永远有进有出，暗示“还有更多”，标题稳稳钉在正中。 | title cards image | editorial technical | mid | 6–12 beats |
| [`poster-tagline`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/poster-tagline/CARD.md) | Poster tagline | Restate the promise at full size in a pause, so it is the one sentence the viewer keeps; the accent word is the person. | 在停顿里用最大的字重说承诺，让它成为观众记住的那一句；强调词就是人。 | title text | dramatic editorial | high | 4–12 beats |
| [`print-read`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/print-read/CARD.md) | Print read | Lift the proof out of the evidence without leaving it: the reading and its source are one object. | 把证据里的数字抬出来读，又不离开证据：读数与它的出处是同一个东西。 | number chart list | technical editorial | mid | 8–24 beats |
| [`reel-titles`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/reel-titles/CARD.md) | Reel titles | Words arrive as frames of the film itself, one pull-down at a time. | 文字作为胶片本身的画格到来，一次拉一格。 | title text | mechanical calm | mid | 2–16 beats |
| [`result-strike`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/result-strike/CARD.md) | Result strike | The proof lands as the music drops: the computed figure, struck like the published ones, and the sentence it proves. | 证明和音乐的 drop 一起落下：算出的数字像发布的数字一样被划出，再加上它证明的那句话。 | metric number text | dramatic editorial | high | 6–12 beats |
| [`sheet-overview`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/sheet-overview/CARD.md) | Sheet overview | Step back to show every sheet was one design, then state the claim with a person's approval on the whole drawing. | 退后一步让人看到每张图都是同一个设计，然后在整张图上写出主张，并由人批准。 | diagram title | ceremonial technical | high | 7–14 beats |
| [`sky-gather`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/sky-gather/CARD.md) | Sky gather | Everything comes back to one place, and only one thing in it needs the user. | 一切回到一处，其中只有一件需要用户。 | abstract ui title | cinematic dramatic | peak | 20–36 beats |
| [`slit-tagline`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/slit-tagline/CARD.md) | Horizon slit tagline | Rush into the claim and then hold it still: the most energy of the film arrives at one line of light that opens to show what it means. | 冲进主张再让它静住：全片最大的能量冲到一条光线上，光线张开，露出这句话。 | title space | cinematic dramatic | high | 8–16 beats |
| [`slot-sentence`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/slot-sentence/CARD.md) | Slot sentence | A promise and its proofs in one line: the stem stays put and only the slot changes, so a list reads as one sentence and ends as a conclusion. | 一句话里装下承诺和证据：句干不动、只换槽里的词，清单读成一句话，最后落成结论。 | text list | elegant calm | mid | 4–14 beats |
| [`thesis-lines`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/thesis-lines/CARD.md) | Thesis lines | The claim is stated in the author's voice with every number the run's own, so it reads as a conclusion, not a slogan. | 结论用作者的口吻说出，每个数字都来自这次运行，读起来是结论，不是口号。 | text | editorial calm | mid | 4–12 beats |
| [`three-angles`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/three-angles/CARD.md) | Three angles | One subject seen whole, closer and in detail, cut in like comic panels on the beat, and the detail grows into a poster that carries the claim. | 同一主体看全貌、近景、细节，像漫画分格一样踩拍劈出，细节那格长成海报托住主张。 | image ui title | energetic playful | high | 9–12 beats |

| [`night-interlude`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/night-interlude/CARD.md) | Night interlude | Keep the film one continuous night past the close, so the film's own section or ending sits in the same sky with no seam. | 让收束之后仍是同一片夜空，片子自己的段落或结尾放在里面，看不出接缝。 | abstract space | calm cinematic | low | 1–64 beats |

| [`pill-dictate`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/pill-dictate/CARD.md) | Pill dictate | A rest between louder beats: the input glows while it waits for you, and gives its light to the words as they arrive, so the request feels calm and personal. | 高能段之间的一个休止：输入条等你开口时亮着光，字一出来就把光让给文字，请求显得安静而亲近。 | voice ui text | calm warm elegant | low | 5–9 beats |

| [`scroll-wall`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/scroll-wall/CARD.md) | Scroll wall | Say 'there is far more than we can show you' in a few seconds: a standing wall of pages keeps flowing past, read as volume, never as detail. | 几秒钟里说清“多到给你看不完”：一面页面墙持续流过，读到的是体量，不是细节。 | image ui cards | calm technical | mid | 8–12 beats |

| [`name-strike`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/name-strike/CARD.md) | Name strike | End on the name, struck like the evidence: the product is the claim the file supports. | 以名字结束，像证据一样被划出：产品就是这份卷宗支撑的断言。 | logo title | editorial ceremonial | mid | 8–16 beats |
| [`neon-marquee`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/neon-marquee/CARD.md) | Neon marquee | Three words the film earned glow like signs on a street at night: a recap with a party mood before the end. | 片子挣来的三个词像夜街招牌一样亮起：带点派对气氛的回顾，收尾前喘口气。 | title space | energetic cinematic | mid | 10–10 beats |
| [`night-interlude`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/night-interlude/CARD.md) | Night interlude | Keep the film one continuous night past the close, so the film's own section or ending sits in the same sky with no seam. | 让收束之后仍是同一片夜空，片子自己的段落或结尾放在里面，看不出接缝。 | abstract space | calm cinematic | low | 1–64 beats |
| [`orbit-title`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/orbit-title/CARD.md) | Orbit title | Hold up one claim with a whole set of real product pictures circling it: cards always entering and leaving say 'there is more', while the title stays fixed in the middle. | 用一整圈真实产品画面托住一句主张：卡片永远有进有出，暗示“还有更多”，标题稳稳钉在正中。 | title cards image | editorial technical | mid | 6–12 beats |
| [`perch-lockup`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/perch-lockup/CARD.md) | Perch lockup | The mascot settles on the name and says where to find the product: a friendly sign-off that leaves an address. | 吉祥物落在名字上并指明产品在哪：友好的道别，顺手留下地址。 | logo title | playful warm | mid | 12–16 beats |
| [`phone-hero`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/phone-hero/CARD.md) | Phone hero | The mobile product, held still yet alive: this is it, and here are the two things to remember. | 移动产品定格但活着：这就是它，再记住两点。 | device ui | calm warm | low | 11–14 beats |
| [`point-lockup`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/point-lockup/CARD.md) | Point lockup | End where everything went: the one point becomes the name. | 在一切归去的地方结束：那一个点变成名字。 | logo title | elegant calm | mid | 14–22 beats |
| [`poster-end`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/poster-end/CARD.md) | Poster end | End on what to type: the name, its parts as a summary, and the URL held long enough to read and remember. | 以“去哪找”收尾：产品名、要点作总结，网址停得够久，读得完也记得住。 | logo title | calm editorial | low | 6–16 beats |
| [`reel-lockup`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/reel-lockup/CARD.md) | Reel lock-up | The reel leaves; what is left on the light box is the name and where to get it. | 胶片走了，灯箱上留下的是名字和去哪里获取。 | title logo image | calm ceremonial | low | 8–20 beats |
| [`release-block`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/release-block/CARD.md) | Release title block | End on the issued drawing: the name, what it does in four words, where to get it, and the status stamped as released. | 以发出的图纸收尾：名称、四个词说明它做什么、去哪获取，以及“已发布”的状态。 | logo title | calm technical | low | 7–16 beats |
| [`roll-lockup`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/roll-lockup/CARD.md) | Roll lock-up | Back to the top, where it began: the name and the address, and the page rolled away. | 回到开头的地方：名字和地址，页面被卷走。 | logo text | calm ceremonial | low | 10–16 beats |
| [`seam-lockup`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/seam-lockup/CARD.md) | Seam lock-up | What to type to get it, beside where it is published; then the seam leaves. | 告诉人怎么装、去哪里看；然后接缝离开。 | title code image | calm ceremonial | low | 10–24 beats |
| [`space-steps`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/space-steps/CARD.md) | Space steps | Three steps told as three places in one space: each chapter gets its own angle, and the pull-back at the end shows how they make one whole. | 把三步讲成同一空间里的三个地方：每章换一个空间视角，最后拉远让观众看到三者合成一个整体。 | cards space title | cinematic elegant | mid | 15–15 beats |
| [`strip-away`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/strip-away/CARD.md) | Strip away | Everything but the one action falls away, so the viewer leaves with that action and the name behind it. | 除了那一个动作，其余都褪去：观众带走的是这个动作和它背后的名字。 | ui cursor logo | calm elegant | mid | 10–12 beats |
| [`thesis-lines`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/thesis-lines/CARD.md) | Thesis lines | The claim is stated in the author's voice with every number the run's own, so it reads as a conclusion, not a slogan. | 结论用作者的口吻说出，每个数字都来自这次运行，读起来是结论，不是口号。 | text | editorial calm | mid | 4–12 beats |
| [`unit-close`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/unit-close/CARD.md) | Unit close | The film ends on the few numbers that matter, standing on a rule made of the same squares, the protagonist last and largest. | 全片收在几个要紧的数字上，它们站在同一批方块排成的尺上，主角最后出现、最大。 | number chart | ceremonial calm | mid | 6–14 beats |
| [`width-name`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/width-name/CARD.md) | Width name | The name breathes with the music: the drop lands it, the bar shapes it, and the dot underlines it. | 名字跟着音乐呼吸：drop 让它落下，小节塑造它，墨点在下面画线。 | logo title | energetic ceremonial | high | 7–10 beats |
| [`word-tunnel`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-tunnel/CARD.md) | Word tunnel | Say 'a lot of things, all here' without asking anyone to read them: the product's nouns stream out of the depth past the camera, so the viewer reads three or five at a time but feels there are many, behind one still title. | 不要求观众读，也能说清“很多东西，全在这里”：产品的名词从纵深飞向镜头并掠过，观众每次只读到三五个，却感到很多，前面压着一行静止的标题。 | text title space | energetic cinematic | high | 8–16 beats |
| [`word-window`](../assets/runtime/bricks/scene/word-window/CARD.md) | Word window | Make the name and the product one picture: the word is a door cut out of the stage, the product is seen through it, and as the word opens the viewer walks from the name into the product. | 让名字和产品成为同一个画面：字是在舞台上挖出的门，产品透过笔画显形，字胀开时观众从品牌词直接走进产品。 | title ui image | dramatic cinematic energetic | high | 11–11 beats |

### Slot: hook

| [`anchor-mark`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/anchor-mark/CARD.md) | Anchor and mark | The brand piece is a printed object: process-ink dots on paper, the black plate round it. | 品牌件是一件印刷物：纸上的印刷色网点，四周是黑版。 | logo | editorial | – | – |
| [`desk-pile`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/desk-pile/CARD.md) | Desk pile | Show "too much at once" as texture before the product takes it all. | 在产品接手之前，先把“同时太多”做成一片质感给人看。 | ui cards cursor | energetic dramatic | – | – |
| [`device-shell`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/device-shell/CARD.md) | Device shell | One device holds every surface honestly: plain hardware claims nothing, and its chin says whose each screen is. | 一台设备如实承载每个画面：朴素的硬件不替产品说话，下巴写明每屏出自哪里。 | device ui | calm technical | – | – |
| [`exhibit-sticker`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/exhibit-sticker/CARD.md) | Exhibit sticker | Say what the page is and where it came from, in the language of a case file. | 用卷宗的说法说清这页是什么、从哪里来。 | text | editorial technical | – | – |
| [`file-slug`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/file-slug/CARD.md) | File slug | Tell the viewer where in the file they are. | 告诉观众现在翻到卷宗的哪里。 | text | editorial calm | – | – |
| [`film-strip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/film-strip/CARD.md) | Film strip | Every picture is a frame on one strip, so order and count are physical. | 每个画面都是同一条胶片上的一格，顺序和数量都是实物。 | image title timeline | mechanical calm | – | – |
| [`glow-thread`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/glow-thread/CARD.md) | Glow thread | Keep one thread on screen through the whole film: whatever arrives, the thread takes it, sends it or brings it back. | 让一根线贯穿整部片：不管来了什么，都是这根线接住、派出或带回。 | abstract space | calm mysterious | – | – |
| [`ink-dot`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/ink-dot/CARD.md) | Ink dot | One mark the eye follows through the whole film. | 一个视线能从头跟到尾的标记。 | cursor abstract | energetic playful | – | – |
| [`mounted-sheet`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/mounted-sheet/CARD.md) | Mounted sheet | Make a capture read as a document on file, with one line of it marked as the claim. | 让截图读起来像归档的文件，并把其中一行标成断言。 | image chart ui | editorial technical | – | – |
| [`paper-term`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/paper-term/CARD.md) | Paper terminal | Show that a machine was asked and answered, in a window light enough to sit on the paper with the rest of the story. | 交代机器被问到、给了回答，窗口够浅，和其余故事一起放在纸面上。 | terminal text | calm technical | – | – |
| [`poster-chrome`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/poster-chrome/CARD.md) | Poster chrome | The film reads as one printed series: the grid shows the system, the header names where you are, the footer counts how far along it is. | 整片读起来是一套印刷系列：网格露出体系，页眉说明身在哪一节，页脚数着走到了哪。 | text abstract | editorial calm | – | – |
| [`print-mount`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/print-mount/CARD.md) | Print mount | Every print gets the same mount, so the evidence is compared like with like and every move rests on one frame. | 每张截图同一种框，证据之间才能同等比较，所有运镜也落在同一个画框上。 | image chart ui | calm editorial | – | – |
| [`query-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/query-line/CARD.md) | Query line | Under every spoken word sits its real parameter, so the viewer believes the question is a real run. | 每个说出的词下面都有它真实的参数，让观众相信这个问题是真跑过的。 | code terminal | technical mechanical | – | – |
| [`quote-band`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/quote-band/CARD.md) | Quote band | Make a line of the page readable without retyping the page: paste the words over it. | 不重排页面也能让一句话读得清：把这句话贴在上面。 | text | editorial calm | – | – |
| [`seam-grip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/seam-grip/CARD.md) | Seam and grip | One boundary the eye can follow from the first caret to the last frame. | 一条眼睛能从第一个光标一直跟到最后一帧的边界。 | code image | technical calm | – | – |
| [`sheet-hud`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/sheet-hud/CARD.md) | Sheet frame | The frame says this is one drawing and where you are in it: sheet n of N, the scale the camera is at, who approved it. | 图框说明这是同一张图、看到了哪：第几张、镜头在什么比例、谁批准的。 | text abstract | technical editorial | – | – |
| [`terminal-hud`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/terminal-hud/CARD.md) | Terminal frame | The frame says this is one terminal session and where you are in it: the tool, its licence, the chapter, how far along. | 画框说明这是同一个终端会话、看到了哪：哪个工具、什么许可、第几章、走到多远。 | terminal text | technical cinematic | – | – |
| [`thread-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/thread-line/CARD.md) | The thread | Keep one object on screen through the whole film: whatever happens, the line was there before and is there after. | 让同一个物体贯穿整部片：不管发生什么，那条线之前在、之后也在。 | abstract cursor timeline | calm technical | – | – |
| [`token-chip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/token-chip/CARD.md) | Token chip | A unit of data looks like a thing that can be merged, counted or tested, and its outline says what is happening to it. | 一个数据单元看起来是能被合并、计数、测试的东西，轮廓颜色说明它正在经历什么。 | code diagram | technical | – | – |
| [`yarn-mascot`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/yarn-mascot/CARD.md) | Yarn mascot | Give the thread a root the viewer can recognise: the product itself, sitting where all the work comes back to. | 给线一个能认出来的根：产品本身，坐在所有事最后回到的地方。 | logo abstract | playful warm | – | – |

| [`poster-chrome`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/poster-chrome/CARD.md) | Poster chrome | The film reads as one printed series: the grid shows the system, the header names where you are, the footer counts how far along it is. | 整片读起来是一套印刷系列：网格露出体系，页眉说明身在哪一节，页脚数着走到了哪。 | text abstract | editorial calm | – | – |
| [`print-mount`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/print-mount/CARD.md) | Print mount | Every print gets the same mount, so the evidence is compared like with like and every move rests on one frame. | 每张截图同一种框，证据之间才能同等比较，所有运镜也落在同一个画框上。 | image chart ui | calm editorial | – | – |
| [`query-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/query-line/CARD.md) | Query line | Under every spoken word sits its real parameter, so the viewer believes the question is a real run. | 每个说出的词下面都有它真实的参数，让观众相信这个问题是真跑过的。 | code terminal | technical mechanical | – | – |
| [`region-light`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/region-light/CARD.md) | Region light | The viewer looks where the film points, and the asset stays exactly as captured. | 观众看向片子指的地方，而素材保持截下来的原样。 | image ui chart | calm technical | – | – |
| [`ruled-ledger`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/ruled-ledger/CARD.md) | Ruled ledger | Let the viewer see the count as a heap of real items, the same heap that will be sorted into the answer. | 让观众把计数看成一堆真东西，后面正是这堆被分成答案。 | list number | calm warm | – | – |
| [`ruled-worksheet`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/ruled-worksheet/CARD.md) | Ruled worksheet | Show the arithmetic, so the result can be checked. | 把算式摆出来，结果可以核对。 | metric number list | technical editorial | – | – |
| [`seam-grip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/seam-grip/CARD.md) | Seam and grip | One boundary the eye can follow from the first caret to the last frame. | 一条眼睛能从第一个光标一直跟到最后一帧的边界。 | code image | technical calm | – | – |
| [`sheet-hud`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/sheet-hud/CARD.md) | Sheet frame | The frame says this is one drawing and where you are in it: sheet n of N, the scale the camera is at, who approved it. | 图框说明这是同一张图、看到了哪：第几张、镜头在什么比例、谁批准的。 | text abstract | technical editorial | – | – |
| [`sheet-minimap`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/sheet-minimap/CARD.md) | Sheet minimap | Show where we are in the page, and the page growing when a row opens. | 告诉观众现在在页面的哪里，展开时看到页面变长。 | ui image | technical calm | – | – |
| [`sketch-node`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/sketch-node/CARD.md) | Sketch node | Make each part feel like a thing drawn for the viewer, distinct from the clean structure around it. | 让每个部件像为观众手画的东西，和周围干净的结构区分开。 | diagram | warm technical | – | – |
| [`terminal-hud`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/terminal-hud/CARD.md) | Terminal frame | The frame says this is one terminal session and where you are in it: the tool, its licence, the chapter, how far along. | 画框说明这是同一个终端会话、看到了哪：哪个工具、什么许可、第几章、走到多远。 | terminal text | technical cinematic | – | – |
| [`terminal-window`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/terminal-window/CARD.md) | Terminal window | A command-line tool shown working as a real object: a command typed, output that settles line by line, and a last line that says it is done. | 把命令行工具当实物来展示它在干活：敲进一条命令，输出一行行落定，最后一行告诉你完成了。 | terminal code | technical mechanical | – | – |
| [`thread-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/thread-line/CARD.md) | The thread | Keep one object on screen through the whole film: whatever happens, the line was there before and is there after. | 让同一个物体贯穿整部片：不管发生什么，那条线之前在、之后也在。 | abstract cursor timeline | calm technical | – | – |
| [`tile-card`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/tile-card/CARD.md) | Tile card | Give a set of things one shared face, so the viewer reads 'a collection of the same kind' and can still tell each member apart by its cover, figure or title. | 给一组东西一张统一的脸，观众读出“这是一类东西的合集”，又能靠封面、图形或标题分清每一张。 | cards image | editorial technical | – | – |
| [`token-bar`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/token-bar/CARD.md) | Token bar | The total the run reduces is always visible, so every step's saving is felt. | 运行在减少的总数一直看得见，每一步省下多少都能感到。 | chart number | technical | – | – |
| [`token-chip`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/token-chip/CARD.md) | Token chip | A unit of data looks like a thing that can be merged, counted or tested, and its outline says what is happening to it. | 一个数据单元看起来是能被合并、计数、测试的东西，轮廓颜色说明它正在经历什么。 | code diagram | technical | – | – |
| [`unit-mark`](../assets/runtime/bricks/prop/unit-mark/CARD.md) | Unit mark | One mark stands for one item all film, so a viewer can count with their eyes. | 一个标记整片代表一件东西，观众能用眼睛数。 | chart | calm technical | – | – |

## Look

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`afterglow`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/afterglow/CARD.md) | Afterglow | Let the ground breathe: the product reads as a source of light in a quiet dark section, without any hit or cut. | 让底色呼吸：在一段安静的暗场里，产品读成一个光源，不靠任何打点或剪切。 | abstract space | calm warm cinematic | – | – |
| [`device-margin`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/device-margin/CARD.md) | Device and margin | A quiet, locked-off frame where the only colour is the product’s own screen and one mark pointing at it. | 安静、固定的画面：唯一的颜色是产品自己的屏幕，加一个指向它的标记。 | device ui image | calm technical | – | – |
| [`diff-paper`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/diff-paper/CARD.md) | Diff paper | Show cause and effect as two sides of one edit: the line written, the result it made. | 把因果呈现为一次编辑的两边：写下的一行，和它做出的结果。 | code image terminal | technical calm | – | – |
| [`drafting-sheet`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/drafting-sheet/CARD.md) | Drafting sheet | A launch drawn as an engineering drawing: every line drafted, the product shown as a designed system, amber only where a person signs. | 把发布画成一张工程图：每根线都是画出来的，产品是被设计出来的系统，只有人签字的地方用琥珀色。 | diagram title | technical mechanical | – | – |
| [`evidence-mat`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/evidence-mat/CARD.md) | Evidence mat | Make the viewer trust a claim by showing it where it was printed, struck like evidence in a file. | 让观众相信一条断言：把它在原出处展示出来，像卷宗里的证据一样划出。 | image chart metric | editorial technical calm | – | – |
| [`light-desk`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/light-desk/CARD.md) | Light desk | A curator’s desk: the evidence is real and untouched, the film only points at it with one pencil. | 策展人的工作台：证据真实、原样，片子只用一支铅笔去指。 | image chart ui | calm editorial | – | – |
| [`live-diagram`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/live-diagram/CARD.md) | Live diagram | A mechanism is believed because it is seen running: the picture is the run's state, with four colours that each mean one thing. | 机制被相信，是因为看见它在跑：画面就是运行状态，四种颜色各只表示一件事。 | diagram code | technical calm | – | – |
| [`neutral`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/neutral/CARD.md) | Neutral | Judge a brick by its motion alone: a plain workbench with no point of view, so nothing but the movement is read. | 只凭动作判断一块积木：一张没有立场的素工作台，画面里除了动作没有别的可读。 | abstract | calm technical | – | – |
| [`newsprint`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/newsprint/CARD.md) | Newsprint | The film reads as printed: one black plate, one process ink kept for the brand piece, and every picture a field of dots that change size, never grey. | 整支片像印出来的：一块黑版，一种只留给品牌件的印刷色，所有画面都是只变大小、不变灰度的网点场。 | abstract logo title | editorial dramatic | – | – |
| [`night-sky`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/night-sky/CARD.md) | Night sky | Make the product's attention one visible thing: a light that runs through a night of loose work. | 把产品的注意力变成一样看得见的东西：在一夜散乱的事里穿行的一道光。 | space ui abstract | calm mysterious technical | – | – |
| [`night-terminal`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/night-terminal/CARD.md) | Night terminal | A launch that looks like the tool running on a glowing screen: the machine speaks in mono, the claim in big lit type, one colour of light. | 像工具在一块发光屏幕上运行那样的发布片：机器用等宽字说话，主张用发光的大字，只有一种光色。 | terminal code title | technical cinematic mysterious | – | – |
| [`one-line`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/one-line/CARD.md) | One line | A motion designer's reel cut as one unbroken transformation, in one line colour. | 像动效设计师的作品集那样，一条线色、一次不断的变形。 | abstract title timeline | playful cinematic technical | – | – |
| [`paper-ledger`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/paper-ledger/CARD.md) | Paper ledger | Make a count feel like evidence kept by hand: every item a dot on paper, every colour a meaning the narrator names. | 让计数像手记的证据：每件事是纸上一颗点，每种颜色是解说点名的一种含义。 | terminal number list | warm editorial calm | – | – |
| [`paper-slam`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/paper-slam/CARD.md) | Paper slam | Type as percussion: every word is a hit on paper, and one dot carries every cut, so the film reads as one gesture. | 把字当打击乐：每个词都是砸在纸上的一下，一个墨点串起每次切换，整支片像一笔写完。 | title text logo | energetic dramatic editorial | – | – |
| [`poster-grid`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/poster-grid/CARD.md) | Poster grid | A product launch set like a Swiss concert-poster series: restraint and a visible system make every step read as decided, and the one colour shows where a person decides. | 把产品发布排成一套瑞士音乐会海报：克制和看得见的体系让每一步都显得经过斟酌，唯一的颜色指出人在哪里做决定。 | title list text | editorial elegant | – | – |
| [`query-ramp`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/query-ramp/CARD.md) | Query ramp | The beat becomes visible: every word lands on a new ground of the same hue, so the film feels like a query run live, and amber can only mean the answer. | 把节拍变成看得见的东西：每个词落在同一色相的新底色上，像一次现场跑的查询；琥珀色只可能是答案。 | title number metric | energetic technical | – | – |
| [`reading-sheet`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/reading-sheet/CARD.md) | Reading sheet | The product's own page is the film: read top to bottom like a long-form story, so every claim is the page's own words. | 产品自己的页面就是影片：像读长文一样从上往下读，每句话都是页面原话。 | text image ui | editorial calm technical | – | – |
| [`reel-box`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/reel-box/CARD.md) | Reel box | Show the work as physical film: real frames you can count, measure and pick by hand. | 把作品当成实体胶片展示：一格一格真实画面，可以数、可以量、可以亲手圈选。 | image title timeline | calm mechanical | – | – |
| [`sketch-paper`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/sketch-paper/CARD.md) | Sketch on paper | Let the viewer keep the whole system in view while one part is explained, in a drawing that looks made by hand for them. | 讲一个部分时让观众一直看得到整个系统，画面像专门为他们手画的。 | diagram terminal | warm technical calm | – | – |
| [`slate-ink`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/slate-ink/CARD.md) | Slate ink | One object visibly becomes the next, so the viewer follows a product's logic as a single continuous thing. | 一个对象看得见地变成下一个，观众把产品的逻辑当成一件连续的东西来跟。 | ui code abstract | elegant technical | – | – |
| [`unit-ledger`](../assets/runtime/bricks/look/unit-ledger/CARD.md) | Unit ledger | Many equal squares on a quiet ground make a count visible: the viewer sees how much before reading a number, and one accent tells them which part the film is about. | 安静底色上一排排相同的方块让数量看得见：观众先看到多少，再读数字；唯一的强调色告诉他这支片讲的是哪一部分。 | chart number | calm editorial technical | – | – |

## Sound

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`data-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/data-bed/CARD.md) | Data bed | Many small landings become a phrase: pitches walk up the scale, so counting sounds like progress. | 许多小落点连成一句：音高沿音阶上行，计数听起来像前进。 | abstract | calm technical | – | – |
| [`desk-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/desk-bed/CARD.md) | Desk bed | Paper and pencil: dry wood under small precise marks, so each ring is heard as a pencil touching the page. | 纸与铅笔：干燥的木质音色托着细小精确的标记，每个圈都像铅笔碰到纸面。 | abstract | calm editorial | – | – |
| [`device-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/device-bed/CARD.md) | Device bed | A soft steady groove under small precise sounds, so each landing on the screen is heard as a click of a real device. | 柔和稳定的律动托着细小精确的音效，屏幕上每次落定都像真设备的一声咔哒。 | abstract | calm technical | – | – |
| [`diagram-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/diagram-bed/CARD.md) | Diagram bed | A quiet bed leaves room to think; each step of the run has its own small sound. | 安静的音乐床给思考留空间；运行的每一步都有自己的小声音。 | abstract | calm technical | – | – |
| [`drafting-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/drafting-bed/CARD.md) | Drafting bed | Sound like a drawing office at work: soft, exact, and with one heavy thud each time something is certified. | 听起来像正在工作的制图室：轻柔、精确，每当有东西被证明就一声沉重的闷响。 | abstract | technical calm | mid | 92–108 BPM |
| [`glass-climb`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/glass-climb/CARD.md) | Glass climb | Energy rises with each dimension, so the climb is heard before the climax. | 能量随每一维上升，高潮之前就听得出在攀升。 | abstract | elegant energetic | – | – |
| [`inkbeat-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/inkbeat-bed/CARD.md) | Ink beat | The type is the percussion: every slam has its stamp, and the two drops are the two things to remember. | 字就是打击乐：每次砸字都有印章声，两次 drop 就是要记住的两件事。 | abstract | energetic dramatic | high | 120–136 BPM |
| [`narration-pad`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/narration-pad/CARD.md) | Narration pad | Keep the room warm without competing with the voice; mark what lands on screen with small sounds. | 让空间有温度但不和人声抢；画面落定处用小声音标一下。 | voice abstract | calm warm | low | 90–140 BPM |
| [`paper-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/paper-bed/CARD.md) | Paper bed | Sound like a desk, not a trailer: restrained paper on a quiet bed, with one drop for the proof. | 听起来像一张办公桌，不像预告片：安静的底子上克制的纸声，只为证明留一次 drop。 | abstract | calm editorial | low | 100–124 BPM |
| [`phosphor-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/phosphor-bed/CARD.md) | Phosphor bed | Sound like a terminal at night with a beat under it: precise keys, clean ticks, one big rush. | 听起来像夜里带着节拍的终端：精确的键声、干净的滴答、一次大的冲刺。 | abstract terminal | energetic technical | high | 120–136 BPM |
| [`poster-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/poster-bed/CARD.md) | Poster bed | Sound that sounds designed, not produced: wood and marimba on a grid of beats, and one bright voice that always means a person deciding. | 听起来是“设计出来的”而不是“制作出来的”：木质与马林巴落在拍子网格上，唯一明亮的声音总是代表人在做决定。 | abstract | editorial calm elegant | mid | 112–128 BPM |
| [`press-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/press-bed/CARD.md) | Press bed | The rhythm of a printing press sets the film in a print shop, and its silence makes the pull-back land. | 印刷机的节奏把片子放进印刷车间，它的静默让拉远落得住。 | abstract | mechanical editorial | – | – |
| [`query-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/query-bed/CARD.md) | Query bed | The pulse is audible: something lands on every beat, the build falls silent for one beat, and the answer hits with the drop. | 节拍听得见：每一拍都有东西落下，高潮前静一拍，答案和 drop 一起砸下。 | abstract | energetic mechanical | high | 112–120 BPM |
| [`reel-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/reel-bed/CARD.md) | Reel bed | A projector’s mechanism under a light reel bed: each pull-down is heard. | 轻快的胶片音乐床下是放映机的机械声：每次拉片都听得见。 | abstract | mechanical playful | – | – |
| [`sheet-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/sheet-bed/CARD.md) | Sheet bed | One long line that keeps moving, like the sheet, with a small sound for every move of it. | 一条一直往前走的长线，像那张长卷，它每动一下都有一个小声音。 | abstract | calm editorial | mid | 112–128 BPM |
| [`shell-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/shell-bed/CARD.md) | Shell bed | The keyboard and the seam are heard: typing, ticks, air as the seam moves. | 听得见键盘和接缝：打字、轻响、接缝移动时的风声。 | abstract | technical mechanical | – | – |
| [`stream-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/stream-bed/CARD.md) | Stream bed | Sound like quiet work at night with a pulse under it: small exact sounds, one alert. | 听起来像夜里安静的工作，底下有脉搏：小而准的声音，只有一次提示。 | abstract terminal | calm technical mysterious | mid | 120–132 BPM |
| [`thread-bed`](../assets/runtime/bricks/sound/thread-bed/CARD.md) | Thread bed | Sound like the chain looks: every landing heard, the energy rising to the sheet and resolving on the name. | 听起来就像这条链的样子：每次落定都听得到，能量升到样片表，在名字上落定。 | abstract | playful cinematic | mid | 112–128 BPM |

## Arc

| Brick | Name | Intent | 意图 | Subjects | Moods | Energy | Length |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [`anchor-launch`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/anchor-launch/CARD.md) | Anchor launch | One piece of the mark holds still while worlds change round it, and the end reveals it was the mark. | 标志的一块一直不动，世界在它周围变换，结尾揭示它就是标志。 | abstract | editorial dramatic | – | 14–40 s |
| [`ask-to-answer`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/ask-to-answer/CARD.md) | Ask to answer | A question the viewer can feel being answered: asked, tried, failed, asked again, answered on the drop. | 让观众感到一个问题被回答的过程：提出、尝试、失败、再问、在 drop 上得到答案。 | abstract | energetic dramatic | – | 12–40 s |
| [`boot-to-end`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/boot-to-end/CARD.md) | Boot to end | One terminal session from the failing command to the URL, the cursor opening and closing it. | 一次终端会话，从失败的命令到网址，光标开场也收尾。 | terminal code title | technical cinematic | – | 30–75 s |
| [`claim-steps`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/claim-steps/CARD.md) | Claim and steps | Argue like a poster series: state the problem, make the claim, prove it one page at a time, and end on the promise and the URL. | 像一套海报那样论证：摆出问题、提出主张、一页一页证明，最后落在承诺和网址上。 | title list diagram | editorial calm | – | 35–60 s |
| [`cold-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/cold-open/CARD.md) | Cold open | Hook the viewer before the film begins: three glimpses of the product's best frames, a breath of black silence, then the name, so the opening says 'this is worth watching' before explaining anything. | 正片开始前先钩住观众：三个产品最抓眼的画面一闪而过，一拍纯黑静默，再亮出名字；什么都还没讲，先让人觉得值得看下去。 | image ui title | dramatic cinematic | – | 3–6 s |
| [`count-then-rule`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/count-then-rule/CARD.md) | Count, then the rule | Earn a rule by counting first: the viewer accepts the conclusion because they watched the items being counted and sorted. | 先数清再立规矩：观众接受结论，是因为亲眼看着东西被数、被分。 | number terminal list | calm editorial warm | – | 60–240 s |
| [`count-to-close`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/count-to-close/CARD.md) | Count to close | A counted set becomes an answer chapter by chapter without ever changing its items. | 一个被计数的集合一章一章变成答案，始终是同一批东西。 | abstract | calm editorial | – | 16–40 s |
| [`desk-survey`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/desk-survey/CARD.md) | Desk survey | A launch argued like a case file: each claim pointed at in its own evidence, the gap measured, the route retraced. | 像卷宗一样论证的发布：每条主张都在它的证据里指出，差距量出来，最后重走一遍路线。 | image chart | calm editorial | – | 24–48 s |
| [`dimension-climb`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/dimension-climb/CARD.md) | Dimension climb | One shape climbs a dimension per scene, so the product reads as one continuous idea. | 一个形状每场升一维，产品读起来是一个连续的想法。 | abstract | elegant technical | – | 12–40 s |
| [`dot-to-claim`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/dot-to-claim/CARD.md) | Dot to claim | One dot, one gesture: from the problem to the claim the viewer follows a single mark. | 一个墨点、一笔到底：从问题到断言，观众只跟着一个标记走。 | abstract | energetic dramatic | – | 20–45 s |
| [`evidence-file`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/evidence-file/CARD.md) | Evidence file | Build trust by citation: every sentence the film says is shown where it was printed, and the one new number is computed in the open. | 靠出处建立信任：片子说的每句话都在原处展示，唯一的新数字当面算出来。 | abstract | editorial technical | – | 15–45 s |
| [`gate-reel`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/gate-reel/CARD.md) | Gate reel | Tell the launch as one reel: words and frames through one gate, the set on one sheet, one pick. | 把发布讲成一卷胶片：字和画格穿过同一个片门，整组排在一张印样上，挑出一个。 | image title | mechanical playful | – | 16–40 s |
| [`line-chain`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/line-chain/CARD.md) | Line chain | Tell the tool's pipeline as one unbroken transformation, each link a step it really performs. | 把工具的流程讲成一次不断的变形，每一环都是它真做的一步。 | abstract timeline title | playful cinematic | – | 12–45 s |
| [`page-read`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/page-read/CARD.md) | Page read | The page is the story: the film only reads it, in order, and opens it once. | 页面就是故事：片子只是按顺序读它，并且打开它一次。 | abstract | editorial calm | – | 16–60 s |
| [`reveal-open`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/reveal-open/CARD.md) | Reveal open | A first impression in three steps: a world before the name, the name held, then the one thing the product is made of. | 三步建立第一印象：先给一个没点名的世界，再亮出名字并停住，最后给出产品由之构成的那一样东西。 | title cards logo | cinematic calm | – | 11–20 s |
| [`rule-run`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/rule-run/CARD.md) | Rule run | The viewer learns a mechanism by watching one step, then believes it from a case it never saw. | 观众看一步学会机制，再从一个没见过的例子里相信它。 | abstract | technical calm | – | 22–40 s |
| [`scatter-gather`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/scatter-gather/CARD.md) | Scatter gather | Tell handing work off as one thread that takes everything, sends it out and brings it all back to one place. | 把“把事交出去”讲成一根线：接走一切、派出去、再全部带回一处。 | abstract ui | cinematic calm | – | 75–120 s |
| [`seam-edit`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/seam-edit/CARD.md) | Seam edit | Tell the launch as edits: every change is a move of one seam between what is written and what it makes. | 把发布讲成一次次编辑：每个变化都是同一道接缝在“写下的”和“做出的”之间移动。 | code image | technical calm | – | 16–36 s |
| [`sheet-route`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/sheet-route/CARD.md) | Sheet route | Argue like a drawing set: one sheet per idea, each a different kind of drawing, signed by a person, checked by machine, approved as a whole. | 像一套图纸那样论证：一张图一个意思、每张是不同类型的图，人签字、机器检查、整体批准。 | diagram title | technical ceremonial | – | 30–60 s |
| [`surface-tour`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/surface-tour/CARD.md) | Surface tour | A launch told only through real screens: the product, then the official evidence, then the product again. | 只用真实屏幕讲的发布：先是产品，再是官方证据，最后回到产品。 | device ui | calm technical | – | 12–60 s |
| [`zoom-return`](../assets/runtime/bricks/arc/zoom-return/CARD.md) | Zoom and return | Teach one part without losing the whole: the viewer leaves and returns to the same map. | 讲一部分而不丢掉整体：观众离开又回到同一张图。 | diagram terminal | calm technical | – | 30–90 s |
